# The ledger of threads (private)

*Every mystery, seed, Chekhov's gun, grudge, promise, prophecy and romance, with where it is planted and where it is paid. Chapter 1 is written and its seeds are marked **planted** below; the payoffs remain planned until their scenes are written. As chapters land, a thread becomes **planted** when its seed is in the text, and **paid** when the payoff scene is written — a thread can be planted and paid more than once, and some payoffs open a new thread in the same line. Plant chapters are planned anchors, reconciled with the day-count in `bible/calendar.md`; the provisional `first_appearance` numbers on character sheets are treated the same way. Chapter 4 is written and closed: the Hearth Drawing, the fifth hearing of the flat note from a person, the second chill with its withheld cause, the two hidden notes and the Drawing collector's reason are in the text and marked against it below. Chapter 5 is written and closed: the quiet morning under the Vail roof where a pot was turned toward and gave nothing, the second ear witnessed and kept silent, the Fishstairs and the market edge, the brown-edged crust and the two cords at Mira's waist are in the text and marked against it below. Chapter 6 is written and closed: the household's arithmetic in loaves, glances and a kindling stack four, four and an empty fifth, a second interval, a fourth sighting of the pause and Alda's second hidden note are in the text. Chapter 7 is written and closed: the last tend and the year's last market, a number carried in a shut fist, a third interval from a cracked pot, a fifth sighting of the pause, two crusts in the fold and a further note toward the tin are in the text and marked against it below; no payoff has been written, and the fifth sighting is a count and nothing more. Chapter 8 is written and closed: the carried fire from an unspoken source, the clean voice and the whole return announced for somebody absent, the told bell kept beside the heard bell, the silence after an unspoken name, the ridge left where it lay and then burned and called dealt with, and the count carried into Y.H. 813 are in the text and marked against it below. Nothing was paid off, no sixth hearing was added, no pause joined, and T088 was opened.*

Chapter 21 is written and closed: Days 101–103, Seed 12–14, Y.H. 813. A neighbour named two absences in one breath and T097 was planted toward the far-off finger that will one day lay a finger on the shelf and call them an account; Berr read one in twelve from the clay road, a neighbour only mentioned the shut counting room, and the boy kept 101, 102 and 103 exactly. Nothing was joined, paid or explained; no seventh hearing came, no third gap, no name and no ending. Ninety-seven threads stand at the close (61 planted, 35 open, 1 paid).

Chapter 22 is written and closed: Days 104–106, Seed 15–17, Y.H. 813. It opened no new thread, paid none and closed none; ninety-seven threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 21 (61 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The direction carried for a month becomes worked ground with hands in it, still counted in glances and handfuls and never in a figure, and a man opens his mouth on the number of hands and thinks better of it, so the district's withholding is performed inside the planting itself. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and four, one hundred and five, one hundred and six kept exactly against a house keeping its days badly), T077 and T015/T041/T047 (one in twelve read from the north road, a number into a slate and not a strip, the speaker taken out of his own sentence, the true subtraction left in the locked box), T058/T079 (instruction to bucket, broom, wool, water, pot and fire, never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; mark untouched at no opening), T009/T013/T092 (worked ground, two unswept lines, new finer grey called the thaw, the space under the lowest rack gone around), T060/T086/T097 (one crust and its unnamed empty place, two shelf gaps in another room, the neighbour's Seed 15 saying filed beside the Seed 12 witness and advanced toward the far-off finger, no joining, no third), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey dust past the bent finger, broken birth-slates under the rack, the damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln), T036/T076/T088 (six counted and the seventh nearly gone and held at the very edge of the sill, chest tight and breath unspent, unforged collector sentence kept, broom over the road without turning, two ears and two silences, loan uncalled; carried fire at four, four and a gap with payer unnamed for a hundred and six days), T059/T025/T024 (Mira's second cord twisted twice beneath the grey, neither knot counted, thin child's knot untouched, board beside Orra's not touching), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set for the uncalled measure, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, no sixth sighting; out-of-line landing walked over without a fall), T023/T089/T007/T008/T073 (Alda's sixth page face-down, six unread, T023 not paid again; both tins shut; the unaware sign, the date over it and the mercy-lie curdled to a fact in a dead woman's face), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, three intervals, four low things in four places with the river's note a fifth, the two tellings joined to no kiln, the word forty unspoken). Withheld hard: no figure for hands or strips, no joining of direction and number, no experiment, rank change, power or Grathic word for the flat note, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no word forty, no name or number from Dessa, no silt-leach cause or cure, no opening of tin, box, slate or widow's mark, no sale, no betrayal paid, no third gap or crust, no ending. Day 107 opens with the seventh year still held and the held breath unspent.

Chapter 24 is written and closed: Days 110–112, Seed 21–23, Y.H. 813. It opened no new thread, paid none and closed none; ninety-seven threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 23 (61 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The planting entered its last tend with the hands still counted in glances and handfuls and never in a figure, and a neighbour at the loaf shelf made a cause of two bare boards in one breath without seeing the empty place at the boy's ear, giving T097 a **third** shaping held beside the two tellings and the watcher's late turn, advanced and **not paid**, with no joining, no name and no third gap. For the first time Tarn's broom checked half a beat behind Jorra's finger standing in the damper, the equality under the roof moving by that much without a word and the loan still uncalled. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and ten, one hundred and eleven and one hundred and twelve kept exact and unspent), T097 (third shaping, not paid), T060/T086 (one crust, two shelf gaps, rooms that do not touch, no third), T058/T079 (instruction to bucket, broom, pot, wool, water and fire, never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; mark untouched at no opening), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called thaw, space under the rack gone around), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among, no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, word forty unspoken, four low things with the river's note a fifth, two tellings of the shut room joined to no kiln), T036/T076 (broom checks half a beat while Jorra's finger stands in the damper --- the first fraction of change in the equality, still unspoken, loan uncalled; seventh year held), T035/T031 (men unnamed before first grey, grey slate dust past the bent finger, broken birth-slates under the rack, promise ungiven), T062 (damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln), T015/T041/T047/T077/T078 (one in twelve, slate not strip, self-removal, fist to nowhere, true subtraction in the locked box), T023/T089 (Alda's sixth page and all three tins shut, **not paid again**), T007/T008/T073 (date over the birth-flower sign, mercy-lie curdled), T059/T025/T024 (Mira's second cord twisted twice, neither knot counted, board beside Orra's not touching), T090/T080 (clerk still set for the uncalled measure, no sixth sighting, no sale), T083 (out-of-line landing walked over without a fall), T088 (carried fire, payer unnamed at a hundred and twelve days), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold, uncured, uncaused, unnamed). Nothing was joined, paid, explained or ended; no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no name or number from Dessa, no third gap and no third crust.

Chapter 25 is written and closed: Days 113–115, Seed 24–26, Y.H. 813. It opened no new thread, paid none and closed none; ninety-seven threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 24 (61 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The planting's last tend holds with the seed up and no figure for the hands that set it; a man brings a number up out of a fist toward the well, looks at it and puts it back in his sleeve, so the figure arrives nowhere; and a neighbour at the loaf shelf lays the shelf's old gap and the empty place at the boy's ear under one cause in a single breath without seeing she has made two rooms one absence, giving T097 a **fourth** shaping held beside the two tellings, the watcher's late turn and the Seed 21 cause, advanced and **not paid**, no joining, no name, no fourth likeness and no third gap. The first fraction of change under the roof made on Seed 22 is **held and not repeated**: Jorra's finger leads at the damper again and Tarn's stroke goes on over the flagstones without the half-check, the stones still keeping the place where it stopped; the two silences stay unjoined and the loan that can hear is still uncalled. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and thirteen, fourteen and fifteen kept exact and unspent), T097 (fourth shaping, not paid), T060/T086 (one crust and its empty place, two shelf gaps, the neighbour's joining, three places that do not touch, no third), T058/T079 (instruction to bucket, broom, pot, fire, wool and water, never the basket; *the house has said it*; half-pace; jar glanced at once and the mark untouched because a shelf is not an opening), T009/T013/T092 (left things kept; two unswept lines called thaw; rack space gone around), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among, no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals plus the unchecking stroke filed beside the earlier half-beat, word forty unspoken with readable double turn and thumb-weight apart, four low things with the river's note a fifth, told bell beside heard, two shut-room tellings joined to no kiln), T036/T076 (knife at the edge, breath from Seed 16 unspent, seventh year held, the first fraction held and not repeated, loan uncalled), T088 (carried fire under blind ash and broken pot, payer unnamed through a hundred and fifteen days), T035/T031 T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms, broken birth-slates under the rack, damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln, *he's not Vail* sealed, promise to the slate-burners ungiven), T077/T015/T041/T047 (figure brought out of a fist and put back, arriving nowhere), T023/T089 (Alda's sixth page and all three tins shut, **not paid again**), T007/T008/T073 (date over the birth-flower sign; *Warm and breathing* exact and not true over a woman more mornings dead than any living person keeps count of), T059/T025/T024 (Mira's second cord twisted twice beneath the grey, neither knot counted, thin child's knot untouched, board beside Orra's not touching), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure, soft-handed man at the Cross holding a figure to a slate at the edge and filed as a sixth shape apart, no sixth sighting, market walking over the landing without a fall, no sale), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold below the sill, uncured, uncaused and unnamed). Foreshadowing planted/advanced: the neighbour's one-breath joining waits with its four shapes for the far-off finger that is not his; the held stroke waits for the later near-saying that stops short of calling the loan; Mira's cords and Orra's knot-string advance toward the convergence of the district's second records; the clerk's uncalled measure and the soft hands advance toward the day a measure set down ahead of a death is understood; Berr's box and true subtraction and the last tend's ungiven cost gather interest toward the hands coming out of the strips in Chapter 26. Nothing joined, paid, named or ended, and the count crosses into Day 116 unspent.

Chapter 10 is written and closed: Days 38–44, Ilmen 9–15, Y.H. 813. Dessa Silt dies on Day 38 in the clay-heavy Slip Brook and stays dead, uncollected; the sixth hearing of the full note comes out of a person, the hand-on-the-basket detail is paid in past tense and untagged, no collector is called and nobody names the omission, a pewter tin is seen above her empty bed, and on Day 44 Orra Weir ties a forty-first knot and says *forty* low into the basket and watches. Two new threads carry what the chapter opened --- the tin above the bed (T089) and the clerk set at the Fishstairs for an uncalled measure (T090) --- while the watched word is folded into T024. T024 moves from open to planted and T087 pays its Day 38 payoff; nothing else is closed, and no hearing becomes a seventh.

Chapter 11 is written and closed: Days 45–47, Ilmen 16–18, Y.H. 813. The boy, forty-five to forty-seven days old, watched a brine-gull carry a not-yet-cold coal off upriver with no slate the poorer; read the shape of the forty-first knot without its name while the word forty went unsaid; and, on the afternoon of Day 46, lost the smoky crust from the fold with nobody saying it was gone or who took it --- the first edge of T086 paid. Frost wrote on the well-lip where the Warm had lately gone out and was called weather by noon; a number went from fist to mouth to shoulder and arrived nowhere; a weak board at the Weir was heard of and not trodden; the clerk at the Fishstairs and the uncollected woman stayed two facts on separate trays. The six hearings stayed six; no seventh came, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no joining of word, shape, cords or trays. T083 moves from open to planted, and T091 is planted over the three hands the boy set on a tray as the possible takers of the crust.

Chapter 26 is written and closed: Days 116–119, Seed 27–30, Y.H. 813, Seventhday–Tenthday, with Seed 30 Account-day and the last market of Seed. It opened **T098**, paid none and closed none; ninety-eight threads stand at the close of Chapter 26 (62 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The planting month goes out with the last hands coming out of the worked strips east of Low Kiln, counted in glances and handfuls and never in a figure --- a woman lifting two chaffed fingers, a man with a barrow shaping the number of backs with his mouth and thinking better of it --- and **one hand that went in on Seed 15 does not come back with the rest**; the yard goes around the empty place in the line with a look and no number, and T098 is planted here: no name, no cause, no accident, no body, no identification of which hand and no payment, held for the far-off day a body is found or a figure is given for what the month cost. The absence is carried through Seed 28, 29 and Account-day 30, where the figure read aloud does not know the hand that did not come back, and is not joined to the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its empty place, or the word forty. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and sixteen, seven, eight and nine kept exact and unspent), T097 (**fifth** shaping on Seed 27 and **sixth** on Seed 29, two tellings of one absence on places that do not touch, advanced and **not paid**, no joining, no name, no fourth likeness and no third gap), T060/T086, T058/T079 (instruction to the wool, the wind, the bucket, the broom, the pot, the fire and the water, never the basket; *the house has said it*; *the house will keep it*; half-pace; jar glanced at once and the mark untouched because a shelf is not an opening), T009/T013/T092 (empty places gone around, two unswept lines called the thaw, rack space and broken birth-slates entered by nobody), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, the unchecking stroke beside the earlier half-beat, the word forty unspoken, four low things with the river's note a fifth, the two tellings of the shut room joined to no kiln), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey dust worked past the bent finger, broken birth-slates under the rack, sealed sentence, promise ungiven; the damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln and read again on Seed 28), T036/T076 (seventh year pressed to the sill's edge and still held, breath from Seed 16 unspent, the unchecking stroke held and not repeated, two silences unjoined and the loan uncalled), T050/T051 (**second glimpse**: green at the corner of a silent woman's mouth filed beside Berr's green and the Silt 30 green, three shapes in three places that do not touch, no name, no dialogue, no ear and not Fenna in the narration), T015/T041/T047/T077/T078 (Berr's twice-wiped thumb, finger over the figure, *one in twelve, as the notice says*, *your house* never *I*, the true subtraction in the locked box, a figure into a slate that is not the figure paid and does not know the missing hand; the bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, ahead of a death; the landing walked over carefully without a fall), T059/T025/T024 (Mira's second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's and not touching, no crust brought and no look into the basket), T023/T089 (six pages unread, no seventh, T023 not paid again; pewter tin, little tin and narrow slate all shut), T007/T008/T073 (Alda's unaware sign covered with a dead woman's date, *Warm and breathing* exact and not true), T093/T094/T096 (the cold foot still dark in three turns of wool, cold to one woman and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused, unnamed) and T088 (the carried fire at four, four and a gap through the floor, its payer unnamed for a hundred and nineteen days, its note kept apart from the river's through the finger's-width door). Nothing was joined, paid, named or ended; no sixth hearing came, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted and no word from Dessa.

Chapter 27 is written and closed: Days 120–122, Reed 1–3, Y.H. 813, Firstday–Thirdday. It opened **T099** and paid none; ninety-nine threads now stand at the close of Chapter 27 with the new row planted and nothing closed. Reed came on without a boundary: the planting's hands went out and the reed work became a direction downriver judged by the arm that carried it, while the missing hand's place in the strip was still gone around with a look and no number (T098 carried, not paid). Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and twenty, one hundred and twenty-one and one hundred and twenty-two kept exact and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted), T058/T079 (instruction to the bucket, broom, pot, fire, wool and river wind and never the basket; *the house has said it* to a woman asking the reeds' cost and to a man asking what the work would want; *the house will keep it*; half-pace; clay jar glanced at once and the mark untouched because a shelf, a strip, a rack, a market and a bed are not openings), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called the thaw, the space beneath the lowest rack and the broken birth-slates entered by nobody), T060/T086 (the shelf's two gaps, one crust and its gap in rooms that do not touch, no third), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground past the bent finger, the full note known before a kiln-master and withheld, the damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise to the slate-burners ungiven), T036/T076 (seventh year pressed past the end of the planting and still held, knife at the very edge of its sill, breath from Seed 16 unspent, unchecking stroke held beside the earlier half-beat with no cause across them, two silences unjoined, loan uncalled), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set for an uncalled measure, hand to slate before eyes to stalls; market sold over him; out-of-line landing walked over without a fall), T033/T034 (**fifth** glimpse of the foundling: head half a beat after the pot, knuckle and note, green sliver rubbed twice, mouth opened on a tail and nothing taken down, no loaf, no slate, no sale, held for the day the delay lands as the physical tell of a betrayal), T059/T025/T024/T023/T089 (Mira's second twist, neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's and not touching; Orra gives a date and gets no answer; six pages in the little tin unread, no seventh; pewter tin shut), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals, the told bell beside the heard bell, the word forty unspoken, the held breath as an object), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused and unnamed), T097 (advanced, not paid, no seventh shaping, no third gap, no joining), T088 (carried fire at four, four and a gap under blind ash with its payer unnamed for a hundred and twenty-two days). **T099 planted** at the Weir on Reed 3: a reed-cutter's tally in wool or reed-fibre gone grey with handling, knots set at the lengths a man's arm could judge and one tied low by teeth and thumb, carried past the boy on the plank walk and not counted, a third private record in a material no Court thumb will read, held for the far-off day it is laid beside Mira's grey cord and second twist, Orra Weir's knot-string, Alda Reed's six pages in the little tin and Dessa Silt's pewter tin above the abandoned bed as the district's complete second account. No figure read, no knot counted, no name, no joining to T098 or to any other record, no tin or box opened and no word forty. Nothing was paid, named, opened, joined or ended.

Chapter 28 is written and closed: Days 123–125, Reed 4–6, Y.H. 813, Fourthday–Sixthday. It opened no new thread, paid none and closed none; ninety-nine threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 27, nothing joined, named, opened, ended or spent. The reed work goes into the beds as the planting went into the strips --- armfuls judged by the arm that carries them, a day's cutting a going-downriver and a coming-back at dusk, the cost asked in the form of a direction and answered with the weather or the river (T071/T072, T077, T058/T079) --- while the yard goes around its two unswept lines called the thaw, the space beneath the lowest rack, the broken birth-slates and the place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come back, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried, not paid). Advanced, none paid: T060/T086 (the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its empty place and the field's gap in rooms that do not touch, no third, no fourth likeness), T097 (two neighbours' joinings on their own places, carried, no third gap, no joining), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln, broken birth-slates under the rack, *he's not Vail* sealed, the promise to the slate-burners ungiven), T065/T095 (the two shut-room tellings kept beside the iron with no third telling), T036/T076 (**for the first time** the sentence about an ear under this roof being worth more than the debt that holds it comes to the threshold of the word that would call the loan and stops there with no figure and no name --- filed as an interval and not a payment on the tray beside Tarn's half-beat at the damper, the unchecking stroke and the boy's own held breath; seventh year pressed past the third tend and still held, knife at the very edge, breath from Seed 16 unspent, two silences unjoined, loan uncalled. Chapter 29 must bring the near-saying once more to the threshold and stop it again), T061/T082 (file laid out, six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, four intervals on one tray and not a rule, the word forty unspoken, three counts in three places that do not become one), T088 (carried fire's missing fifth breath, payer unnamed for a hundred and twenty-five days), T099 (carried again on Reed 6, cord straightened on the thumb, one knot tied low with teeth, put back inside the coat, knots unread, no joining to T098 or to the word forty), T059/T025/T024 (Mira's second twist shown beneath the grey, neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching; Orra gives the ninth of Ilmen and a collector's boat with six oars and one man at the stern who never touched them where a levy figure was asked, a date with the question taken out, word forty unspoken), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set for an uncalled measure, market sold over him, out-of-line landing walked over without a fall), T015/T041/T047/T077 (Berr's twice-wiped thumb, *A number goes into a slate, not into a bundle*, one in twelve as the notice says, self-removal with *your house*, never *I*, true subtraction in the locked box), T023/T089 (Alda's six pages and Dessa's pewter tin carried, not opened), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused, unnamed), T004 (old life held offstage, no present-tense break and no tagged memory). T050/T051 were **not** advanced: the green-mouthed woman was offstage and only Berr's tasted glaze was glimpsed; a third green is owed in Chapter 29. Nothing was paid, named, opened, joined or ended, and the count crosses into Day 126 unspent.

Chapter 29 is written and closed: Days 126–128, Reed 7–9, Y.H. 813, Seventhday to Ninthday. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; ninety-nine threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 28 (63 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is the **second stopping**: under the Vail roof Tarn brings the sentence about an ear under this roof being worth more than the debt that holds it all the way to the word that would call the loan a second time and stops there with no figure and no name, drawing the breath he should have spent and not spending it; the boy files it on the interval tray beside the first as a **second interval and not a second payment**, not a hearing and not a seventh, and the loan stays uncalled with the seventh year pressed further past its third tend and still held (T036/T076, T061/T082). Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and twenty-six, seven and eight kept exact and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted), T077 (reeds judged by the carrying arm, cost asked as a direction and answered with the pot, the weather or the river), T058/T079 (instruction to wool, river wind, bucket, broom, pot and fire and never the basket; *the house has said it*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace from every open slate; widow's mark untouched at a rack, strip, well, yard, market, road, basket, bed and dusk because none is an opening), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called the thaw, the rack space and broken birth-slates entered by nobody, the place in the strip gone around with a look and no number), T098 (carried through Reed 7–9, gone around with a wide step, no name, cause, body, figure or payer), T060/T086 (two shelf gaps in one room, one crust and its empty place in another, no third, no joining), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, bent finger in the cold damper ahead of an unfinished sentence, damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise ungiven), T065/T095 (two shut-room tellings kept beside the iron with no third), T015/T041/T047/T077 (Berr's folded red cord, green at one mouth-corner, thumb wiped twice before touching nothing, *a number goes into a slate, not into a bundle*, one in twelve read, levy called and denied with *your house*, never *I*, the speaker taken out of his own sentence, true subtraction still in the locked box), **T050/T051 (a THIRD green glimpsed at a silent woman's mouth at the market on Reed 7 — clay under her nails, a pale line on the back of her hand where something had once been worn tight and long, a cup turned a quarter and set back — held on a place of its own beside Berr's glaze and the Silt 30 and Seed 28 greens; three at mouths and four shapes counting Berr's; no name, no dialogue, no ear, no cause and not Fenna in the narration)**, T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, the told bell beside the heard bell, word forty unspoken, two stoppings and the earlier objects on the interval tray as objects and not a rule), T088 (carried fire at four, four and a gap under blind ash and a broken pot, fifth breath missing, payer unnamed for a hundred and twenty-six, seven and eight days), T099 (reed-cutter's grey cord straightened on the thumb, knots stood, one tied low with teeth and bitten off, put back inside the coat; *You can ask the cord* and *No more can the Court*; carried and not counted, no joining to T098 or the word forty), T024/T059/T025 (Orra's legible unnamed forty-first knot and her ninth of Ilmen with six oars and the man at the stern who never touched them, Mira's grey cord and second twist twice beneath it, neither knot counted, thin child's knot untouched, board beside Orra's not touching, word forty unspoken), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure, cuff grey from washing, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, market sold over him, out-of-line landing walked over sideways without a fall), T007/T008/T023/T089 (Alda's sign crossed unknowing, covered with the date she gave Dessa, *Warm and breathing* exact and not true over a woman more mornings dead than any living person keeps count of, tin touched unopened, six pages unread, T023 not paid again), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused and unnamed), T097 (carried and unpaid, no third gap, no finger laid, no joining), and T004 (old life held offstage: no present-tense Chicago break, no tagged remembered line). Nothing was joined, named, paid, opened, called, counted, figured or ended: no calling of the loan, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted, no word forty, no sale, no tin or box opened, no body or cause for the missing hand. The charcoal tally under the Vail loaf shelf remains **unplanted** and belongs to Chapter 30. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 30 is written and closed: Days 129–131, Reed 10–12, Y.H. 813, Tenthday to Secondday, with Reed 10 the first Account-day of Reed. It opened **T100** and paid none; one hundred threads now stand at the close of Chapter 30 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid) with nothing joined, named, opened, called or ended. The chapter's one meaningful change is the **charcoal tally on the UNDERSIDE of the Vail loaf shelf**, planted in Scene 2 and carried in Scene 7: short strokes in grouped clusters, some pressed hard, some faded nearly into the grain, a keeping put where nobody thinks to look above a row kept badly on top. The boy saw it from the basket's low angle and did not read, count, copy or show it, did not lay it beside the two gaps above, the fold's one crust, Alda's tin, Mira's cords, Orra's knots, Dessa's pewter tin or the reed-cutter's cord; on Day 131 he saw Sella's finger go beneath the board and come away without a word and made no cause of the habit. T100 is planted and unpaid, held for the far-off day a hand not his lays the district's private records together, and must not be read, counted or joined in Chapters 31–33. The near-saying came a **third** time on Reed 11 to the threshold of the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name; Tarn drew the breath he did not spend, the boy filed a third interval and not a third payment, refusing to let three stoppings stand up and call themselves a doctrine, and the loan stayed uncalled with the seventh year still held. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (129, 130 and 131 kept exact by the only person who would have known what the number counted), T015/T041/T047/T077 (one in twelve read over the first Account-day crowd, figure read not figure paid, true subtraction in the locked box, self-removal with *your house*, never *I*), T078 (heard bell beside told bell, four, four and a gap, nobody crossing himself), T090/T080/T083 (clerk still set for an uncalled measure, cuff grey from washing and grey again by midday, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, market sold over him, landing walked over carefully), T058/T079 (instruction to wool, river wind, pot, broom and fire, never the basket; half-pace from every open slate; *the house*; *Ask the pot*; widow's mark untouched at a shelf, an afternoon and a dusk), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called the thaw, rack space and broken birth-slates entered by nobody, missing hand's place gone around with a look), T098 (carried on Reed 10, gone around at the edge of the reading with no name, cause, body, figure or payer), T060/T086 (two shelf gaps, one crust and its empty place in rooms that do not touch, no third), T097 (carried and unpaid, no finger laid, no joining), T035/T031/T062 (three or four men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, broken birth-slates under the rack, *he's not Vail* sealed, promise ungiven, damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln), T065/T095 (two shut-room tellings beside the iron with no third), T036/T076 (**third stopping withheld**, seventh year held, knife back on its sill with six counted where the house can check and the seventh held where it cannot, breath unspent, unchecking stroke, two silences unjoined, loan uncalled), T061/T082 (three stoppings as objects and not a rule, six hearings six, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, no seventh, word forty unspoken), T088 (carried fire's missing fifth breath, payer unnamed for 129, 130 and 131 days), T099 (reed-cutter's cord tied low with teeth, bitten off, put back unread), T024/T059/T025 (Orra's legible unnamed forty-first double turn and her ninth of Ilmen with six oars and a man at the stern who never touched them, Mira's second twist twice beneath the grey, neither knot counted, thin child's knot untouched), T023/T089/T007/T008 (Alda's six pages and both tins shut, sign covered with a dead woman's date, mercy-lie curdled further), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot still the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, extremity going before the core read and bought nothing), T050/T051 (no green glimpsed this chapter beyond Berr's own; the four greens stay three at mouths and four shapes counting his, no name and not Fenna), T004 (old life offstage: no present-tense Chicago break, no `\memory{}` tag, no power used and no rank change). Nothing was joined, named, paid, opened, called, counted, figured or ended: no calling of the loan, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no word forty, no sale, no tin or box opened, no body or cause for the missing hand. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 31 is written and closed: Days 132–134, Reed 13–15, Y.H. 813, Thirdday to Fifthday. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 30 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The reed month's middle tend is lived one dated day at a time, and the chapter gathers rather than resolves: **T036/T076** advances one interval further as Tarn brings the sentence about an ear worth more than the debt that holds it a **fourth** time to the word that would call the loan and stops there with no figure and no name, draws the breath he does not spend and goes on with the broom; the boy files a **fourth stopping on the interval tray as an object and not a rule, an interval and not a payment**, refusing to let four likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine (a doctrine is an account that has found a payer), and the loan stays uncalled with the seventh year still held. **T050/T051** gathers a **fourth green at a mouth and the fifth shape of the colour counting Berr's glaze** --- a silent woman at the pot board with clay under her nails and a pale cord-mark on the back of one hand, unnamed, silent, given no dialogue and never Fenna in the narration, turning a cup a quarter and setting it back --- filed beside the Silt 30, Seed 28 and Reed 7 greens on places that do not touch, a colour and not a name and joined to no ear. Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (132, 133 and 134 kept exact and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted), T077 and T058/T079 (bundles judged by the arm that carries them; *Your arm'll tell you what went into it, and that's the whole of what it knows*; cost asked as a direction answered with the weather and the river; *Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it*; instruction to wool, river wind, pot, broom and fire and never the basket; *the house*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace; widow's mark untouched at a shelf, an afternoon, a strip, a rack, a basket, a bed and a dusk because none is an opening), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called the thaw, rack space and broken birth-slates entered by nobody, the empty place in the returning line gone around with a look and a wide step and no number), T098 (carried through Reed 13–15), T100 (carried, unread: Sella's finger beneath the Vail loaf shelf on Days 132 and 134, without looking and without a word, the charcoal still separate from the two gaps above and the one crust in the fold), T099 (carried: the reed-cutter's grey cord straightened on the flat blessing stone, one knot tied low with teeth and the end bitten off and put back inside his coat, *You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court*, knots unread), T024/T059/T025 (Orra's date with the six-oared boat and the man at the stern who never touched them, the word forty unspoken with the forty-first double turn legible; Mira's second twist twice beneath the grey, neither knot counted, thin child's knot untouched, board beside Orra's not touching), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey into the finger's-width-open back room, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, *It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady*, pot told *You'll do*, full note withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master who said it was singing before its time, bent finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, the double bite filed joined to no kiln, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise of the name ungiven), T065/T095 (two shut-room tellings with no third), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, four stoppings among the intervals as objects and not a rule, told bell beside heard bell), T090/T080/T083 (soft-cuffed clerk still set for an uncalled measure, cuff grey from washing and grey again by midday, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, a figure entered before the man who said it was looked at, market sold over him, out-of-line landing walked over without a fall), T088 (carried fire's four low pulls and missing fifth breath, payer unnamed for 132, 133 and 134 days; three counts of river, fire and boards kept in three places that do not become one), T015/T041/T047/T077 (Berr's folded red cord, thumb wiped twice, two marks short for two pair of eels, *A number goes into a slate, not a bundle*, *your house* never *I*, true subtraction still in the locked box), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot dark in three turns of wool below the sill, the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, the extremity going before the core read perfectly and buying nothing, no silt-leach, cause or cure), T060/T086/T097 (one crust and its unnamed empty place, the shelf's two gaps in a room that does not touch, no third, no joining, no finger laid), T023/T089 (Alda's six pages and both tins and Dessa's pewter tin shut, offstage), T078 (told bell beside heard bell). Nothing was joined, named, paid, opened, called, counted, figured or ended: no calling of the loan, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no word forty, no sale, no tin or box opened, no body or cause for the missing hand, and the old life stayed offstage throughout --- no present-tense Chicago break, no `\memory{}` tag, no power used and no rank change. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 32 is written and closed: Days 135–137, Reed 16–18, Y.H. 813, Sixthday to Eighthday. The loan stays uncalled; the seventh year presses nearer its end and has still not given. Chapter 33 is written and closed: Days 138–140, Reed 19–21, Y.H. 813, Ninthday to Firstday, with Reed 20 the second Account-day of Reed. The loan stays uncalled; the seventh year presses nearer its end and has still not given. A second gathering: on the second Account-day a **fifth green** came to the mouth of a silent woman across the square at the pot board --- clay under her nails, a pale line across the back of one hand, a cup turned a quarter and set back exactly where it stood --- unnamed, silent and given no dialogue, filed beside Berr's glaze and the Silt 30, Seed 28, Reed 7 and Reed 15 greens as the fifth at a mouth and the sixth shape of the colour, a colour and not a name, never Fenna in the narration and joined to no ear (T050/T051, advanced, not paid). *the house*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace from every open slate; widow's mark untouched at a shelf, a strip, a rack, a market, a basket, a bed and a dusk, because none was an opening), T009/T013/T092 (two unswept lines called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates entered by nobody, the empty place in the returning line gone around with a look and no number), T098 (carried through Reed 19–21, no name, cause, body, figure or payer, not joined to the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust or the word forty), T099 (the reed-cutter's grey cord turned so its knots stood, one tied low with teeth, bitten off and put back inside his coat on Days 138 and 140, *You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court*, no knot counted, no figure read, no joining), T100 (Sella's finger beneath the loaf shelf on Days 138 and 140, without looking or a word, the charcoal tally still unread, uncounted and separate from the two gaps above and the one crust in the fold), T060/T086 (two shelf gaps in one room, one crust and its empty place in another, no third, no joining), T097 (carried, no neighbour named the two absences in these three days, no third gap, no finger laid), T036/T076 (sixth stopping withheld, loan uncalled, seventh year pressed nearer its end, breath from Seed 16 unspent, unchecking stroke held beside the earlier half-beat with no cause across them, two silences unjoined), T061/T082 (sixth stopping as an object on the interval tray and not a rule; river's note, fire's gap and boards' knot still three counts in three places that do not become one). Nothing was joined, paid, named, opened, called, spent or ended: no calling of the loan, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted, no word forty spoken, no name or number from Dessa, no sale, no opening of tin or box, no body, cause or figure for the missing hand, no reading of either private tally, no third gap and no fourth likeness made into a cause. The district's private records --- charcoal under the shelf, the reed-cutter's cord, Orra's knot-string, Mira's second twist, Alda's six pages and both tins, Dessa's pewter tin, Berr's locked box and the soft-cuffed clerk's inward slate --- gather toward a far-off hand not his that will lay them together and call the joining an account; the fifth green gathers toward a far day an ear will be spoken with that colour; the sixth stopping and the pressed seventh year gather toward the day something must give. Held for the next chapters: T097 advanced and unpaid, T098/T099/T100 carried and unpaid, the fifth green a colour and never Fenna in narration, the foundling offstage with no sixth glimpse and no sale, Marra not in Sill, Dessa dead and unnamed to the boy, *he's not Vail* sealed and the foundling's promise ungiven.

*Warm and breathing* exact and not true), T023/T089 (six pages turned toward a floorboard, both tins and the pewter tin above the abandoned bed shut, T023 not paid again), T078 (told bell beside heard bell, kept apart), T088 (carried fire's four low pulls and the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for one hundred and forty-four, forty-five and forty-six days), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, eight stoppings as objects on the tray). T050/T051 held five greens as colours and not names and no sixth green came; T015/T041/T047 and T033/T034 were held offstage with no sale, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand and no sixth glimpse of the watcher. A second gathering, not a resolution: the charcoal under the shelf, the reed-cutter's cord, Orra's knot-string, Mira's two cords, Alda's six pages and both tins, Dessa's pewter tin and the soft-cuffed clerk's inward slate go on being kept by hands that cannot read them, side by side and not across, gathering toward a far-off hand that will lay them together and call the joining an account, with no payer named to any. No new thread was opened and no payoff was written; the sixth green and the sixth glimpse of the watcher are owed at the last market of Reed in Chapter 36, and the count crosses into Day 147 unspent.

Chapter 36 is written and closed: Days 147–149, Reed 28–30, Y.H. 813, Eighthday to Tenthday, with Reed 30 the third Account-day and the last market of Reed. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 35 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The reed month goes out without a boundary drawn and the last market stands with every withholding in one square and none of them read: a figure read aloud that is not the figure paid, a cord no Court thumb can spell, a clerk set for a measure nobody called, a sixth green that is still only a colour and a foundling's sixth half-turn that is still only a shape. The chapter's one meaningful change is the **ninth stopping**: under the Vail roof on Day 148 Tarn tells the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* and brings the sentence about an ear under this roof being worth more than the debt that holds it all the way to the word that would call the loan — on Reed 29 after Reed 5, 8, 11, 14, 17, 20, 23 and 26 — and stops there with no figure and no name, draws the breath he does not spend, says *Yes, house* to the fire and goes on with the broom. The boy files the ninth stopping on the interval tray beside eight as a ninth interval and not a ninth payment, an object among objects and not a rule, refusing to let nine likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine, for a doctrine is an account that has found a payer and no payer had been named to any of the nine; the loan stays uncalled and the seventh year stands one tend from its end and has not given (T036/T076, T061/T082). A second gathering, not a resolution: at the last market of Reed a **sixth** green comes to a silent woman's mouth at the pot board and a **sixth** silent glimpse of the foundling comes at the market's edge, both filed as a colour and a shape on places of their own, never joined, never Fenna in narration, the foundling unnamed and given no dialogue, no loaf passed, no slate seen and no sale (T050/T051, T033/T034). Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and forty-seven, forty-eight and forty-nine kept exact and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted), T077 (costs asked as directions answered with the weather, the river, the pot and the house, leaving no entry), T058/T079 (instruction to wool, river wind, pot, broom and fire and never the basket; *the house*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace; widow's mark untouched at a shelf, an afternoon, a strip, a yard, a market, a bed and a dusk because none is an opening), T009/T013/T092 (clean dark line, thinner grey called the thaw, broken birth-slates under the rack and the empty place in the returning line gone around with a look and no number), T098 (carried through Reed 28–30, no name, cause, body, figure or payer, and the figure read at the last market does not know the hand that did not come back), T099 (reed-cutter's grey cord straightened and turned on the blessing stone, one knot tied low with teeth, end bitten off, put back inside the coat with knots unread; *You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court*), T100 (Day 147 finger beneath the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies, without looking or a word, brought away unread), T024/T059/T025 (Orra's forty-first double turn legible with the word forty unspoken; Mira's second twist twice beneath the grey, neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching), T093/T094/T096 (cold foot below the sill the cold to one woman and weather to another, uncured, uncaused, unnamed, and the boy's perfect reading worth nothing), T090/T080 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure, hand to slate before eyes to stalls), T015/T041/T047 (Berr wiped his thumb twice, read *one in twelve, as the notice says*, called the levy and denied it with *your house*, never *I*, paid two marks short into an uncounted palm and went off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness), T078 (bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, the full note withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master and the damper's double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln), T065/T095 (two shut-room tellings with no third), T088 (carried fire's four low pulls and the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for a hundred and forty-nine days), T060/T086/T097 (two shelf gaps in a room the fold does not touch, one stiff crust in the fold with its empty place unnamed, and no third, no joining and no finger laid). No new thread opened; the ninth stopping, the pressed seventh year, the sixth green and the foundling's sixth half-turn gather interest into Smoke and are not spent. The charcoal under the shelf, the reed-cutter's cord, Orra's knots, Mira's two cords, Alda's six pages and both tins, Dessa's pewter tin, Berr's locked box and the clerk's inward slate still stand side by side for the far-off hand that will lay them together and call the joining an account.

Chapter 37 is written and closed: Days 150–152, Tallow 1–3, Y.H. 813, Firstday to Thirdday. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 36 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). **Calendar reconciliation:** `plan/next.md` and the close of Chapter 36 called these days Smoke 1–3; the binding `bible/calendar.md` rule (*t* = 336 + *N* − 1) gives **Tallow 1–3**, and this chapter's scenes, meta and beat sheet all use Tallow. The kiln-firing texture is kept; the first Account-day and market-day of the month is Tallow 10 (Day 159) and belongs to Chapter 38. The chapter's one meaningful change is the **first opening** under the Vail roof: for the first firing of Tallow Sella put her hand to the widow's mark *before* the kiln mouth, held it one breath, let go, tipped the carried coal under the back of a broken pot and did not touch the mark again at a shelf, a well, an afternoon or a dusk --- a real opening after a hundred and forty-nine days of going around, with the rule held exactly and nobody to praise it or see it broken. The fire answered four low pulls and the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for a hundred and fifty, a hundred and fifty-one and a hundred and fifty-two days (T058/T079, T088). A second texture, not a resolution: the shallow clay pan that stood mouth-up against the wall through all of Reed is gone with no scrape, crumb, print, chip, word or dust disturbance, and the boy holds its round empty place on a tray of its own, apart from the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its gap, the field's empty place, the sixth green and the sixth shape; no third gap came, no fourth likeness was made a cause and no finger of his drew the line (T060/T086, T097 held, advanced and unpaid). Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and fifty, one hundred and fifty-one and one hundred and fifty-two kept exact and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted, told by nobody), T009/T013/T092 (the clean dark line where the Ash ridge turned, the new finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates under the lowest rack and the trough by the well now without its skin of ice gone around or called weather, entered by nobody), T098 (carried on Days 150 and 151: the place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out gone around with a look and a wide step and no number), T035/T031/T062 (men unnamed before first grey into the finger's-width-open back room, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and under the nails, not bone-coloured slip, dusty and clean forearms kept on one tray, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master who said the pot was singing before its time and withheld, the knowing going to the near-full tray and not among the hearings, the bent finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, one iron with two bites filed joined to no kiln with no Grathic word for both mercies, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise to the slate-burners ungiven), T065/T095 (two tellings of the shut counting room held beside the damper with no third, though men went into a room that stands a finger's width open), T036/T076 (**the coming of the sentence on Day 152 is not a tenth stopping:** at the going of the light Tarn told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count*, and the sentence about an ear under this roof being worth more than the debt that holds it came up as it had come up nine times before but on this day was **not** brought to the word that would call the loan --- it stopped short of the threshold, no tenth stopping stood up to join the nine, the breath that should have gone in at the top of the reach was drawn and not spent, and he said *Yes, house* to the fire and went back to the broom; the knife at the very edge of its sill, the unforged collector sentence beside it, the seventh year one tend from its end and still held, two ears in their equality for a hundred and fifty-two days, two silences unjoined and the loan uncalled), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh and none invented, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, the older intervals with the half-beat, the unchecking stroke, the held breath and the unchecking stroke, and **nine** stoppings as objects and not a rule, intervals and not payments, no payer named to any; a tenth standing up before the seventh year is earned would be a debt invented to make the nine into a bill, and the absence of a tenth is a keeping and not a payment), T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket; *the house*; *The house will keep it*; *Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it*; half-pace from every open slate; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand; the mark touched once before the opening and at no other hour), T077 (costs asked in the form of directions answered with directions --- the going down and the coming back, the river, the pot --- and leaving no entry), T099 (reed-cutter's grey cord straightened on the flat blessing stone, turned once so the knots stood, tied low with teeth and a pull of the thumb, bitten off and put back inside the coat with the flat of the hand, answering *You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court*; knots unread, carried and unpaid), T024/T059/T025 (Orra's ninth of Ilmen with the boat of six oars and the man at the stern who never touched them and her forty-first double turn legible with the word forty unspoken; Mira's *Thin? Who's to say thin? It comes when it comes. Ask the river* and *Two marks the pair and two short*, her thumb to the grey cord and the second twist twice beneath it with neither knot counted and the thin child's knot untouched, her board beside Orra's and not touching; three private records within a dozen paces of the basket and none entered), T100 (Day 151: standing with her back to the basket and moving her lips along the row with two gaps, Sella reached a finger BENEATH the front edge where the charcoal tally lies, without looking or a word, brought it away and did not go back; such a hand either knows the board by heart or has been told not to look, and the reason stands between the two; marks unread, uncounted, unjoined), T090/T080 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing below the north bridge for a measure nobody called, cuff grey from washing and grey again by midday, slate turned inward and hand to slate before eyes to stalls; the market sold over him as water goes over a stone and a girl with a creel took the sideways step without a fall), T093/T094/T096 (child's foot below the sill dark in three turns of wool, the cold to the woman who wound it and weather to the neighbour with a pail; the extremity going before the core, the almost-smile that is not peace and the afterdrop waiting past a warming nobody would perform read perfectly and worth nothing; no silt-leach, cause, cure or recognition), T050/T051 (sixth green and sixth half-turn landed at the last market of Reed and held offstage here as a colour and a shape with no loaf, no slate and no sale; never Fenna in narration and never named), T033/T034 (foundling offstage all three days, the sixth glimpse gathering interest and spent for no effect), T023/T089 (Alda's six pages and both tins, Dessa's pewter tin above the abandoned bed and Berr's locked box named in the night file but neither on stage nor opened; T023 not paid again), T078 (told bell of the Long Night beside the heard bell, four, four and a gap in two places that do not meet), T004 (old life held offstage: no present-tense Chicago break, no `\memory{}` tag, no power used and no rank change; the surgeon's register colours the theatre of the opening, the two bites of one iron, ground dust against mixed slip, the pan's gone place, the five record-first hands, the nine likenesses held apart, the cold foot and the file laid out like instruments without breaking the boy's eyes). The carried fire missed its fifth under the floor for a hundred and fifty, one hundred and fifty-one and a hundred and fifty-two days while the kiln breathed in fours behind the wall and the river's note came through the finger's-width door, three counts in three places that did not become one. The first opening plants the firing month's danger --- the breath that takes a man in his sleep, dust ground small as meal, a pot that sings before its time --- for the days to come; the nine stoppings, the pressed seventh year, the damper's double bite and the two shut-room tellings gather toward the day something must give and a payer be named at the foot of the account. Nothing was joined, paid, named, opened, called, spent or ended: no calling of the loan, no tenth stopping, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted, no word forty, no name from Dessa, no name for the green, no body, cause or figure for the missing hand, no reading of any private tally, no tin or box opened, Marra not in Sill, *he's not Vail* sealed, the foundling's name promise ungiven and the old life offstage. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 38 is written and closed: Days 153–159, Tallow 4–10, Y.H. 813, Fourthday to Tenthday, with Tallow 10 the first Account-day and market-day of the firing month. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 37 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is the arrival of a new named character with a real role, **Hessa Rale** of Slate Row, a slate-runner who carries Berr Clay's narrow slate edge-on between the Cross and Greybank. On Day 158 her hand goes to the slate before her eyes go to the yard, and on Day 159 she takes it up the clay road with hand to slate before eye to the crowd; the boy files that order of reaching as an **eighth record-first hand-shape** on a place of its own — not Marra, not the neat dark hand that set a name before a face, not the Court-table clerk, not the soft-cuffed clerk at the landing, not the soft hands at Slate Row, not Berr's twice-wiped thumb, not the finger beneath the Vail loaf shelf. She is a runner and not a reader of any private keeping, no figure comes off her slate, and no joining is made (T080 advanced and **not paid**; the T080 summary rows are corrected to match). The first Account-day of Tallow goes up the clay road in Berr's locked box with the printed figure unchallenged and the true subtraction inside, and a figure read aloud is still not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047, T077). Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and fifty-three through one hundred and fifty-nine kept exact, complete and unspent by the only person who would have known what the number counted, five months and three days to five months and nine days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and fifty-three through a hundred and fifty-nine days, the fire breathing behind the wall in fours and missing its fifth under the floor); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket; *the house*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace; the widow's mark untouched at a shelf, a well, an afternoon and a dusk because none is an opening; cost sent to the pot with *The house has said it*); T062 (damper as one iron with two bites, the holding and the taking, joined to no kiln, Jorra's finger standing in it ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence and his knowing withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master on the near-full tray); T061/T082 (nine stoppings as objects and not a rule, advanced and not paid, with **no tenth stood up** in these seven days — the near-saying did not come to the threshold at all, so nothing was paid and no payer was named); T036/T076 (seventh year pressed nearer its end than ever and still held, knife at the very edge of its sill, Seed 16 breath unspent, unforged collector sentence beside it, no collector on the road, loan uncalled); T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, broken birth-slates under the rack, two shut-room tellings with no third); T009/T013/T092 and T098 (two unswept lines called thaw, rack space and broken birth-slates entered by nobody, the place where a hand went in on Seed 15 gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T099 (reed-cutter's cord straightened on the blessing stone, one knot tied low with teeth and bitten off, knots unread, no joining); T024/T059/T025 (Orra's ninth of Ilmen and the boat of six oars with the man at the stern who never touched them laid where a figure was asked, word forty unspoken; Mira's second twist twice beneath the creel with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's and not touching); T093/T094/T096 (child's foot dark in three turns of wool below the fish-trade shade, cold to one woman and weather to the yard, surgeon's reading perfect and worth nothing, no silt-leach, cause or cure); T090 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing, cuff grey by midday, slate inward, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, for a measure nobody called); T015/T041/T047/T077/T078 (one in twelve read over the first Account-day crowd, self-removal with *your house*, never *I*, the heard bell beside the told bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself); T060/T086/T097 (one stiff brown-edged crust two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps in the other room, the pan's gone place on its own tray, no joining, no fourth likeness made a cause); T100 (Sella's finger beneath the loaf shelf's front edge on Day 157, without looking and without a word, the charcoal unread); T050/T051 and T033/T034 (held: sixth green a colour joined to no ear, sixth foundling glimpse a shape on a place of its own; never Fenna in narration, no sale, no loaf, no seventh glimpse); T004 (old life offstage: no remembered line, no present-tense Chicago break, no departure from the boy's eyes). Nothing joined, named, spent, sold or ended.

Chapter 39 is written and closed: Days 160–162, Tallow 11–13, Y.H. 813, Firstday to Thirdday, the firing month's second working week. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 38 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is a fraction, not a settlement: on Day 161, for the first time under the Vail roof, Jorra's bent finger went to the cold damper and stopped until Sella had finished *It wants feeding*, then moved the tongue a hair's breadth and left it standing. The household straightened around a sentence that had been allowed to finish and nobody said a word of the waiting; the boy filed it on a place of its own as a change held and unspoken --- not a reconciliation, not a hearing, not a payment, no payer named and no slate entered --- and kept it apart from Tarn's unchecking stroke and the nine stoppings. **This is a movement of T062/T035/T031 and T061/T082, not a new thread and not a payment.** *He's not Vail* stays sealed, the promise to the slate-burners stays ungiven, no bond changed hands and the loan stays uncalled. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and sixty, one hundred and sixty-one and one hundred and sixty-two kept exact, complete and unspent, five months and ten days to five months and twelve days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed through 162, the kiln breathing in fours with a gap and missing its fifth under the floor); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket; *the house*; *The house will keep it*; half-pace; the widow's mark untouched at a morning, a shelf, a well, a rack, a road, a bed and a dusk; the half-beat at setting; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand; the two rooms that do not touch; costs sent to the pot without a figure); T009/T013/T092 and T098 (the clean dark line, the new finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the field's empty place gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and held apart from bone-coloured slip, broken birth-slates under the rack, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln with no Grathic word for both mercies, the two shut-room tellings with no third); T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh and none invented, three near-full answers advanced and not paid with Jorra's knowing among them, the older intervals, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no payer and no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the told bell beside the heard bell, three counts in three places that do not become one); T036/T076 (seventh year nearer its end than it has ever stood and still held, six counted where the house can check and the seventh where it cannot, knife at the very edge, Garl's unforged collector sentence beside it, Seed 16 breath unspent, ear-sentence to the teeth and no further, loan uncalled, no collector coming, two silences unjoined); T060/T086/T097 (one stiff brown-edged crust two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps not one gap twice, the pan's gone place on its own tray, the sixth green a colour and the foundling a shape, no fourth likeness made a cause and no finger drawing the line); T100 (charcoal clusters under the shelf's front edge, Sella's finger beneath the board on Day 160 without looking or a word, the reason standing between knowing the row by heart and being told not to look); T077 (cost asked as a direction answered with the river, the weather or the pot, the arm that carries the reeds keeping weight and not cost); T099 (reed-cutter's cord straightened on the blessing stone, turned once, tied once low with teeth and hidden inside his coat, *You can ask the cord* and *No more can the Court*, knots unread); T024/T059/T025 (Orra's date at the small flat knock of a thing laid where it belongs, kept apart from the knock of a vessel cracked through, the forty-first double turn legible and the word forty unspoken; Mira's *Ask the river*, her eel-bones while deciding whether to lie, her second twist twice beneath the creel, her board beside Orra's and not touching); T093/T094/T096 (the child's dark foot in three turns of wool, extremity before core, a cold vessel that cannot clot, no rubbing and no fire, the afterdrop waiting past a warming nobody performs, exact and worth nothing at two paces); T090/T080 (the soft-cuffed clerk still set for an uncalled measure, slate inward, market sold over him, the girl with a creel taking the landing sideways with no fall made of it); T006 (the warm stone in Tarn's clay pot in the loft, given by Sella in the winter Garl died, held as a detail and not spent); T004 (old life offstage: no remembered line, no present-tense Chicago break, no departure from the boy's eyes). Nothing joined, named, spent, sold, read or ended.

Chapter 40 is written and closed: Days 163–169, Tallow 14–20, Y.H. 813, Fourthday to Tenthday, the firing month's third working week and its second Account-day, a passage chapter at the seven-day cap. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 39 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is a held beat, not a settlement: on Day 166, under the Vail roof at the loaf shelf, Sella's finger went BENEATH the front edge where the charcoal tally lies and stayed for the length of one of the breaths she counts before she brought it away --- she did not look, read, copy, give a word or go back to the shelf that night. The boy filed the held beat on a place of its own as a change held and unspoken --- not a reading of the charcoal, not a payment, no payer named --- and kept it from standing up with the Day 161 fraction at the damper and the older intervals into a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer. **This is a movement of T100 and T061/T082, not a new thread and not a payment.** Nothing was joined: the shelf's two gaps, the fold's crust and its gap, the pan's gone place and the field's empty place stayed on separate trays, no third gap came and no fourth likeness was made a cause. *He's not Vail* stays sealed, the promise of the foundling's name ungiven, no bond changed hands, no debt paid and the loan uncalled. The Day 169 sighting of Hessa Rale with her slate edge-on and her figure unread is an advancement of T080, **not a payment**. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and sixty-three through one hundred and sixty-nine kept exact, complete and unspent, five months and thirteen days to five months and nineteen days, told by nobody); The next I don't count*, ear-sentence to the teeth and no further, loan uncalled, no collector coming, two silences unjoined); T060/T086/T097 (one stiff brown-edged crust two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps not one gap twice, the pan's gone place on its own tray, the sixth green a colour and the foundling a shape offstage, no fourth likeness made a cause and no finger drawing the line);
