Nothing joined, named, spent, sold, read or ended.

Chapter 41 is written and closed: Days 170–172, Tallow 21–23, Y.H. 813, Firstday to Thirdday, the firing month's last working week. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 40 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is a **third movement of the equality under the Vail roof**, held and unspoken: on Day 171, while Jorra's bent finger stood in the cold damper and Sella's unfinished sentence was allowed to finish, Tarn's broom checked half a beat behind the standing finger on the flagstones and went on without a word or a turn of the head — the Seed 22 half-check returned changed after a hundred and fifty days, not a reconciliation, a hearing, a stopping, a payment or a mercy, with no payer named, no slate entered, the loan uncalled and no tenth stopping stood up. The boy filed it on a place of its own **beside** the Day 161 fraction at the damper and the Day 166 held beat beneath the loaf shelf: three held changes on three places, and he forbade them to stand up and call themselves a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer. **This is a movement of T062/T035/T031, T036/T076, T061/T082 and T100, not a new thread and not a payment.** Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and seventy through one hundred and seventy-two kept exact, complete and unspent, five months and twenty days to five months and twenty-two days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and seventy through a hundred and seventy-two days, the kiln breathing in fours with its gap behind the wall and missing its fifth under the floor through the firing month's last tend); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never to the basket, *the house*, half-pace from every open slate, widow's mark untouched at a morning, a shelf and a dusk, the half-beat at setting, the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand, the two rooms that do not touch, costs sent to pots, weather and rivers with no figure); T009/T013/T092 and T098 carried (the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T035/T031 and T062 (men unnamed before first grey, grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and held apart from bone-coloured slip, broken birth-slates under the rack, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln and no Grathic word for both mercies); T061/T082 (six hearings of the full note beside and never among, five from four sources and the sixth from a person, with no seventh and none invented; three near-full answers advanced and not paid, with Jorra's withheld knowing among them and not among the hearings; the older intervals at Stone 13, 17 and 25; nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no payer named to any, no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled); T036/T076 (six counted where the house can check and the seventh held where it cannot, the small knife at the edge of its sill, Garl's unforged collector sentence, the ear-sentence to the teeth on Day 172 and no further, the warm stone cold in its pot in the loft); T077 (costs asked as directions answered with the pot, the weather and the river, leaving no entry); T065/T095 (two shut-room tellings side by side and no third); T060/T086/T097 (the shelf's two gaps, the crust and its gap, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on separate trays, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause); T004 (old life offstage, no remembered line, no present-tense Chicago break, no departure from his eyes). T100 carried and **not** advanced into a reading: the Day 166 held beat beneath the loaf shelf stayed where it was left, not repeated on Days 170–172, Sella's finger not going beneath the board, the charcoal unread and awaiting a hand that is not his and a day that has not come. T050/T051, T033/T034, T023/T089, T090/T080, T093/T094/T096 and T099 stayed offstage or unspent. Gathered only, nothing paid: the sweetish lead edge, the pot singing out of time and the dust ground small as meal toward the small non-fatal edge owed later; the three held fractions toward the day a rule will try to stand up out of likenesses; the seventh year's approaching end and the ninth stopping toward the end of the term; the fire's unnamed payer toward Sella's second roll; the unread charcoal toward the far-off hand that will lay the district's private records together; Tallow 30's third Account-day toward Chapter 42.

Chapter 42 is written and closed: Days 173–179, Tallow 24–30, Y.H. 813, Fourthday to Tenthday, a passage chapter at the seven-day cap. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 41 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). The chapter's one meaningful change is the third Account-day of Tallow lived and read over the boy on Day 179, the last market of the firing month: Berr Clay read *one in twelve, as the notice says* for the third time in the month, wiped his thumb twice, said *The levy is called. It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, and went off up the clay road with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness; the district's arithmetic changed for the month and cannot be undone, while the printed figure stands unchallenged and a figure read aloud is still not the figure paid. Sella set the basket in the moot-post's finger's width of shade where no slate lay and stood half a pace off, a displacement made with no word and no reason given to the basket, and carried him home at the going of the light saying nothing. Nothing else was settled: no payer named, no slate opened, no bond changed hands, no debt paid. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and seventy-three through one hundred and seventy-nine kept exact, complete and unspent, five months and twenty-three days to five months and twenty-nine days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed on all seven days, the kiln breathing in fours with its gap through the last tend, the month going out without a boundary drawn); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket, *the house*, half-pace from every open slate, the new displacement into the moot-post's finger's width of shade with no reason given, widow's mark untouched because no hour among these seven was an opening, half-beat at setting, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand, costs sent to the pot and carried away level, two rooms that do not touch); T009/T013/T092 and T098 carried (clean dark line, finer grey called thaw, rack space with broken birth-slates, pan's gone ring and field's empty place gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T035/T031, T062 and T065/T095 (men unnamed into the back room on Days 174, 176 and 178, grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and held apart from mixed slip, full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln and no Grathic word for both mercies, bent finger held in the iron until the sentence finished on Days 174 and 178, two shut-room tellings with no third); T036/T076 and T061/T082 (six counted where the house can check and the seventh at the lip, Garl's sentence beside the knife, the ear-sentence at the teeth on Days 173, 176 and 178 and no further, Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, loan uncalled, no collector coming, nine hearings beside and never among with no seventh and none invented, the sixth from a person filed beside and never among, three near-full answers advanced with Jorra's knowing among them, older intervals at Stone 13, 17 and 25); T060/T086, T097 and T100 carried (shelf's two gaps, fold's crust and its empty place, pan's gone ring and field's empty place on separate trays, charcoal waiting under the board for a hand that is not his and a day that has not come, Sella's finger never going beneath it on any of the seven days); T062/T100/T036/T076 (the Day 161 damper fraction, the Day 166 held beat and the Day 171 flagstone half-check kept on three separate places, never repeated as new changes, never paired or stood up as a rule with a payer); T078 carried (heard bell beside told, four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, spending its last strokes at the going of the light on Day 179); T015/T041/T047 and T077 carried (figure read not paid, *your house*, box locked, cost sent to the pot as a direction arriving nowhere); T080 advanced (Hessa's record-first reaching as the eighth such hand in the district, filed on a place of its own, carried and not read, advanced and not paid); T090, T050/T051 and T033/T034 held (soft-cuffed clerk set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure with his inward slate and hand-to-slate-before-eye order; sixth green a colour joined to no ear at a silent woman's mouth, never Fenna in narration; foundling a shape with no sale, offstage, sixth glimpse unspent, no loaf and no slate near the basket). Gathered only, nothing paid: the dust ground small as meal, the sweetish lead edge on a wrong wind and the pot singing before its time toward the non-fatal edge owed later; the three held changes and the nine stoppings toward a day a rule will try to stand up out of likenesses; the held seventh year toward a later giving; the fire's unnamed payer toward Sella's second roll and the Hearth as a wound; the two shut-room tellings and the damper's double bite toward ~650; the locked box, twice-wiped thumb, inward slates, cords, knots, charcoal and empty places toward the far-off hand that will lay the district's private records together; the green and the foundling toward their far-off payings. Nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended; *he's not Vail* sealed, the foundling's name promise ungiven, Dessa dead and unnamed, Marra out of Sill, and the old life offstage with no `\memory{}` tag, no present-tense Chicago break and no departure from the boy's eyes. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 43 is written and closed: Days 180–182, Ember 1–3, Y.H. 813, Firstday to Thirdday. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 42 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). Ember came on without a boundary drawn in the narration, the district keeping its own word for Tallow the firing month and Ember the kiln month, neither corrected; there was no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage. The chapter's one meaningful change is the **first firing of Ember** on the morning of Day 180: Sella put her hand to the widow's mark before the kiln mouth, held it one breath, lifted the clay lid with the back of a hand and tipped the carried coal under the back of a broken pot, and the fire took, still answering four low pulls with the fifth missing and no payer named, nobody praising the opening and nobody seeing what it mended. She did not touch the mark again at a shelf, a well, a rack, a road, a basket, an afternoon or a dusk on any of the three days, because the hand goes to the mark before the kiln and at no other hour; the rule held exactly as on Tallow 1. This is a real opening performed under the household's roof — **T058/T079 advanced, not paid**, no slate entered, no lesson made, no bond changed hands, nothing else settled. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and eighty, one hundred and eighty-one and one hundred and eighty-two kept exact, complete and unspent, six months exactly on the first morning to six months and two days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty, a hundred and eighty-one and a hundred and eighty-two days, the kiln breathing in fours with its gap behind the wall and missing its fifth under the floor through the opened kiln month, no boundary drawn between Tallow and Ember); T058/T079 (instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never the basket; *the house*; half-pace from every open slate; one glance at the clay jar cold under its glaze without a touch; the widow's mark touched once before the kiln and at no other hour; the half-beat at setting; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand; two rooms that do not touch; costs sent to the pot and carried away level); T009/T013/T092 and T098 carried (clean dark line, finer grey called the thaw, rack space with broken birth-slates, bare dark trough whose water is called weather, pan's gone ring and field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T035/T031/T062 and T065/T095 (men unnamed into the back room before first grey on Days 181 and 182, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal under the nails and white in the creases where a fist opened, not bone-coloured slip, dusty forearms and clean ones kept on one tray with neither question asked, broken birth-slates entered by nobody, full note known three turns ahead before a High Kiln kiln-master on the wrong wind and withheld with no pot lost, the knowing going to the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them, the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln and no Grathic word for both mercies, Jorra's bent finger held in the iron until Sella's sentence reached the pot on Day 181 and remembered on Day 182 without being a new change, the two shut-room tellings with no third); T036/T076 and T061/T082 (Tarn told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* on all three days, six counted where the house can check and the seventh held at the lip where it cannot, Garl's unforged collector sentence beside the knife, the ear-sentence at his teeth on Days 180, 181 and 182 and no further, the breath drawn and not spent, the late thaw wanted because it puts off the paying, broom over the collector's road without turning and no collector coming, Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, loan uncalled, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh invented and none invented to stand up as a seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, intervals at Stone 13, 17 and 25, the river's note a fifth low thing never counted among the hearings); T060/T086/T097 (one stiff brown-edged crust two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps in a room that does not touch the fold, pan's gone ring and field's empty place on separate trays, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause and no finger of his drawing the line); T100 (charcoal under the shelf's front edge unread, uncounted and uncopied, Sella's finger not going beneath the board on any of the three days, the Day 166 held beat not repeated); T062/T035/T031/T036/T076 (the Day 161 damper fraction, the Day 166 held beat and the Day 171 flagstone half-check kept as three held changes on three separate places, never repeated as new changes, never paired or stood up as a doctrine or a rule with a payer); T077 (cost asked of the newly opened firing sent to the pot, the weather and the river with no figure, question carried away level leaving no entry; the danger of the month given an order with no name that cannot be carried into a chart — hot clay, slate dust ground small as meal, sweetish lead glaze on a wrong wind, a pot set down wrong giving its high thin note before its time which the woman who set it down does not hear — filed as a danger and made into no lesson); T004 (old life offstage: no remembered line, no present-tense Chicago break, no departure from the boy's eyes; the surgeon's register colours the opening as a procedure with a body, a tool and a price, the carrying arm as a record in muscle that keeps weight and not cost, the private file turned out as instruments on a towel, the three likenesses taken down one at a time). Gathered only, nothing paid: the dust ground small as meal, the sweetish lead edge on a wrong wind and the pot singing out of time toward the small non-fatal edge owed later, with no pot lost and no lesson made; the unnamed fifth toward Sella's second roll and the Hearth as a wound; the damper's two bites and the two shut-room tellings toward their distant paying; the nine stoppings and the pressed seventh year toward the later calling of the loan; the unread charcoal, the two shelf gaps, the crust's gap, the pan's gone ring, the field's empty place, the cords, knots, tins, box and inward slates toward the far-off hand that will lay the district's private records together. No seventh hearing, no tenth stopping, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause; *he's not Vail* sealed, the foundling's name promise ungiven and the foundling offstage, the sixth green a colour and the sixth shape offstage, Marra out of Sill, Dessa dead and unnamed, Alda's mercy-lie uncorrected, the loan uncalled and the warmth of the stone in the loft unreconciled and not forgiven. Nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 44 is written and closed: Days 183–185, Ember 4–6, Y.H. 813, Fourthday to Sixthday, a normal three-day chapter in the kiln month's second working week. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 43 (64 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). There was no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage, and the district's two names for the months went uncorrected. The chapter's one meaningful change is Tarn's taking the warm stone out of its unmarked clay pot in the Debt Loft on the afternoon of Day 183 — two years gone and cold, not looked at in a year — holding it, setting it on the sill where the finger's-width opening let in light, looking at it a long moment and putting it back. No word, no figure, no payer, no slate; he did not reconcile the stone with the debt and did not forgive it, for not trying is not forgiving, and the debt stands exactly as it stood. **This is T006 advanced and T036/T076 advanced, not paid.** From below the boy heard only the small dry scrape of a pot's foot set down on stone from a room he cannot enter, and filed it on a place of its own, not joining it to the Day 161 damper fraction, the Day 166 held beat beneath the shelf or the Day 171 flagstone half-check and not letting a fourth likeness stand up and call itself a cause, for a rule is an account that has found a payer. On Days 184 and 185 he filed the remembering of the holding as a remembering and not a holding made again. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and eighty-three, four and five kept exact, complete and unspent, six months and three days to six months and five days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-three, four and five days, the kiln breathing in fours with its gap through the second working week and missing its fifth under the floor); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket, *the house*, half-pace, the clay jar glanced at cold under its glaze without a touch, the pot handle set two inches beyond the reaching hand with the half-beat at setting, costs sent to the pot and the weather, the one opening still behind the household — the mark touched once before the kiln on Ember 1 and at no shelf, well, rack, road, basket, afternoon or dusk since — and the two rooms that do not touch); T009/T013/T092 and T098 (the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round place and the field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T035/T031, T062 and T065/T095 (three or four men unnamed before first grey on Days 184 and 185, grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms and white in a knuckle when a fist opens, not bone-coloured slip, the dusty forearms and the clean ones kept on one tray, the full note known three turns ahead and withheld from a man on a wrong wind who said a pot singing early generally sings itself out early, the knowing going to the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them with no pot lost, Sella's unfinished sentence finished at the cold damper while Jorra's bent finger stood in it and moved the tongue a hair's breadth — a remembered holding on the later days and not a new change — the iron still one piece with two bites joined to no kiln and with no Grathic word for both mercies, and the two shut-room tellings kept at two with no third); T036/T076 and T061/T082 (six counted in the worn thumb-mark where the house can check and the seventh held at the lip where it cannot, Garl's unforged collector sentence beside the knife, the ear-sentence short of the word that would call the loan on all three days, the Seed 16 breath unspent with its small interval at the top of every reach, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the loan uncalled, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh invented, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, the three held changes on three separate places, the told bell beside the heard, and a pot's high thin note before its time filed as a danger and not promoted into a fourth near-full answer); T100 (the finger kept from beneath the loaf shelf on all three days and the charcoal still unread, uncounted and uncopied, the Day 166 beat left where it was); T060/T086 (the crust gone leathery two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place kept on trays that do not touch, with no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause); T077 (a neighbour's cost sent to the pot and the weather on Ember 5 and left with no entry, the dust, the wrong wind and the early note gathering toward the small non-fatal edge owed in Chapter 45, with no edge paid and no lesson made); T004 (the old life entirely offstage — no `\memory{}` tag, no present-tense Chicago break and no departure from the boy's eyes, the surgeon's register reading the whole as an inventory that is exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces). Nothing was joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended; no payer was named, no seventh hearing came, no tenth stopping stood up and no third gap appeared.

Chapter 45 is written and closed: Days 186–188, Ember 7–9, Seventhday to Ninthday, Y.H. 813, a normal three-day chapter in the middle of the kiln month, every day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid) — T064 moved from open to planted when the sweet-sickness was at last written in a body on Days 187–188, its earlier in-world note in Y.H. 641 not counting as a planted seed. There was no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage, and the district's two names for the months went uncorrected. **The chapter's one meaningful change is paid small and non-fatally and entered nowhere:** on Day 188 the sweetish edge of lead glaze rode a wrong wind off High Kiln, a rack-worker set a pot down wrong and the pot gave its high thin note before its time and then sang itself out early with the woman going on with her hands and making no lesson of it, and two men carried a glaze-room hand out of the kiln and across the yard toward the clay road, alive and breathing, the sweet-sickness on his breath, the pale line at his gum, his own hand limp with fingers the colour of old rope and pewter-pale nails, one carrier's thumb hooked under his arm to keep the head from lopping. Sella set the basket at the yard's edge, turned the boy's face as she would turn a pot to the light and went wide around the place the carriers crossed, giving no name for what ailed him; no collector was called, no figure given, no payer named and no slate opened, and the cost stayed entered nowhere. The boy read the carried body exactly as a man with lead in him, in the surgeon's register — the line at the gum, the sweet breath, the way the hand was held, the thumb under the arm, the colour of the nail beds — and could not reach, speak or spend any of it; he was the only adult in the yard and the least use in it. He filed the pale line on a place of its own: not a hearing, not a seventh, not a fourth near-full answer, not a stopping, not one of the three held changes and not a fourth likeness stood up to call itself a cause, for a rule is an account that has found a payer. **This is T077 advanced and not paid as a thread, and T061/T082 and T064 advanced**, a single edge spent and entered nowhere, not a payoff of any long mystery; the High Kiln saying about a pot singing early was not made true as a lesson, the pot was swept around, the man went on breathing up the road and no offstage death is to be invented. Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and eighty-six, seven and eight kept exact, complete and unspent, six months and six days to six months and eight days, told by nobody); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for 186, 187 and 188 days, the kiln breathing in fours with its gap behind the wall and the fire missing its fifth under the floor through the kiln month's third working week, no boundary drawn between Tallow and Ember); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket, *the house*, half-pace, clay jar glanced at cold under its glaze without a touch, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with the half-beat at setting, two rooms that do not touch, costs sent to pot and weather with nothing slopped, priced, refused or entered, the widow's mark touched once before the kiln on Ember 1 and at no shelf, well, rack, road, basket, afternoon or dusk since, the boy's face turned as a pot is turned on Day 188); T009/T013/T092 and T098 carried (the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round place, the bare dark trough whose water is called weather and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, gone around with a look and a wide step and no number on all three days, including around the place the carriers crossed); T035/T031, T062 and T065/T095 (men unnamed before first grey on Day 187, grey slate dust ground small as meal and parted from slip as a clot from its vessel, the full note known three turns ahead and withheld from a man on a wrong wind, the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln and no Grathic word for both mercies, the bent finger held in the cold iron on all three days as a remembered repetition and not a new change, the two shut-room tellings still two with no third, the promise of the foundling's name ungiven); T036/T076 and T061/T082 (six counted in the worn thumb-mark where the house can check and the seventh held at the lip where it cannot, Garl's unforged collector sentence beside the knife, the ear-sentence at the threshold of the word on all three days and stopped with no figure and no name, the Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule with no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the loan uncalled, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, three held changes on three separate places with the loft-sill scrape on a fourth, the told bell beside the heard, early and out-of-season notes filed as dangers and not promoted); T100 (charcoal unread under the loaf shelf, Sella's finger kept from beneath the front edge on all three days, the Day 166 held beat unrevived); T006 held (the stone back in its pot in the loft, cold and unreconciled, the remembering filed as a remembering and not a holding made again, not stood up with the three held changes); T060/T086 (one stiff brown-edged barley crust gone leathery two inches from the ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the shelf's two unnamed gaps, the frozen thread, the rooms that do not touch, no joining and no fourth likeness); T077 (a neighbour's cost sent to the pot and the weather and arriving nowhere, the sweet wind, the early note and the pale line filed as dangers, no entry and no payer); T004 (the old life stayed offstage, no present-tense break and no tagged `\memory{}` line, the surgeon's register only colouring the noticing). Nothing was joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, no seventh hearing came, no tenth stopping stood up, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause and no ending was written.

Chapter 46 is written and closed: Days 189–191, Ember 10–12, Y.H. 813, Tenthday to Secondday, a normal three-day chapter with Ember 10 the first Account-day and market-day of the kiln month, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** Ember's first Account-day is read over the basket and the district's arithmetic changes for the month and cannot be undone. Berr Clay comes up short off the clay road with the red office cord folded square under his coat and green glaze at one mouth-corner, wipes his thumb twice before touching nothing, holds a narrow slate unopened to his forearm, passes a finger over the figure, reads *one in twelve, as the notice says* low into the goers-by, says *The levy is called. It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, and goes off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he has done a kindness; the printed figure stands unchallenged and a figure read aloud is still not the figure paid. The reading does **not** answer the man carried out on Day 188: no figure, collector or slate knows what he cost, and the pale line stays on a place of its own. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and eighty-nine, ninety and ninety-one kept exact, six months and nine days to six months and eleven days, told by nobody); T088 (the fire's payer unnamed through all three days, four low pulls under the floor with the fifth missing, three counts in three places that do not become one); T015/T041/T047/T077 (the first reading of Ember, self-removal, box locked, figure read not figure paid, costs sent to the pot and the weather and arriving nowhere); T078 (heard bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, kept beside the told bell); T080 (Hessa's slate up the road to Greybank edge-on and unread, advanced and not paid); T090 (soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called); T050/T051 and T033/T034 (the sixth green still a colour joined to no ear, never Fenna, the foundling still a shape with no sale and **not** counted as a seventh sighting, the no-seventh-in-Chapters-46–50 rule honoured); T061/T082 and T064 (told figure beside paid figure, told bell beside heard, the pale line on its own place, the sweet wind and the early note filed as dangers and not promoted into hearings); T009/T013/T092/T098 (the yard gone around its unswept places and the place the carriers crossed with a look and a wide step and no number); Chapter 47 is written and closed: Days 192–194, Ember 13–15, Y.H. 813, Thirdday to Fifthday, a normal three-day chapter in the kiln month's second working week, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close as stood at the close of Chapter 46 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). The account has gone away up the road in Berr's locked box and nothing under the Vail roof changes hands; the three quiet days gather rather than resolve. **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** in the small hours of Day 194 the boy's hand outside the wool goes up an inch toward the note of water over water through the finger's-width door and, for the first time, does not come down at once — it stays up for the length of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire in the room that does not touch his (small intake, hold, letting go), then comes down against the fold. There were two lesser fractions on the way: on the afternoon of Day 193 the hand went up and hesitated for the length of the small pause in the kiln's breath before coming down, and on the morning of Day 194 it went up and came down at once. The held inch is filed on a place of its own **beside** the others and never laid across them — not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment, not one of the three held changes, not a key, not a power and not a rule, with no payer named and no doctrine stood up out of it. **This is a movement of T071/T072 and T061/T082, a physical first and not a new thread and not a payment**; he still cannot close the two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin to the leathery crust or speak a number, and an inch held twice starting to look like a reaching is the whole of what the saga will make of it. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (one hundred and ninety-two, ninety-three and ninety-four kept exact, six months and twelve days to six months and fourteen days, spoken by nobody under any roof; he has stopped expecting to be told what a number counts, which is not the same as not wanting it); T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for 192–194 days, four low pulls under the floor with the fifth missing, the river's note through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four, and a knot — three counts in three places that do not become one); T077 (cost-questions about the firing, the pots and a wet thing set down in the dry heat sent to the pot and the weather and carried away level, nothing priced, refused or entered); T015/T041/T047/T080/T090 (the printed one in twelve still unchallenged, the true subtraction out of reach in the locked box up the road, Hessa's slate delivered unread at Greybank and the soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing — all held and offstage, brought on only as what has gone away); T058/T079 (instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom and running water, never the basket; *the house* and *The house will keep it*; half-pace; jar-glance without a touch; gruel from the house's own dish with his mouth unwiped; the half-beat at setting; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand; the widow's mark untouched at every morning, shelf and dusk because a quiet morning after a reading is not an opening and there has been no opening since Ember 1; the two rooms that do not touch — hers with a pot, a fire and a woman counting toward nothing entered, his with a crust two inches from an ear and an empty place beside it with no name); T009/T013/T092/T098 (the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough whose water is called weather, the field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and the place the carriers crossed, each gone around with a look and a wide step and no number); T064 (the sweet edge on a wrong wind taken as a direction, the pot that sang early swept around and not made an omen or a lesson so soon after the carrying-out, no collector called, no figure given, no sickness named in Grathic and no slate opened for the man carried out on Day 188, his pale line held on a fifth place across nothing and his breath kept, which is not an ending); T036/T076 (six counted where the house can check and the seventh at the lip where it cannot, the knife on its sill with Garl's unforged sentence beside it, the ear-sentence brought to the threshold of the word that would call the loan and stopped there with no figure and no name on all three days, the breath drawn and not spent, nine stoppings still nine objects with no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the loan uncalled, and the stroke over the flagstones past the bright patch without the half-check); T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (men unnamed before first grey, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal and parted from the slip as a clot from its vessel, dusty and clean forearms on one tray, the full note known three turns ahead and withheld from a man on the wrong wind with no pot lost, the bent finger standing in the cold damper as a remembered repetition and not a new change, the damper's two bites joined to no kiln, the two shut-room tellings at two with no third, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise of the boy's name to the slate-burners ungiven); T006 (the stone cold and unreconciled in its unmarked pot in the loft, the scrape heard from below filed as a remembering and not a holding made again and not joined to the three held changes); T100 (charcoal unread beneath the shelf and Sella's finger kept from beneath its front edge on all three days); T060/T086/T097 (the leathery crust two inches from the ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps in the other room, the pan's ring and the field's empty place on trays that do not touch, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause, no finger of his drawing the line); T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with the sixth out of a person and no seventh invented, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard, and now the held inch filed on its own place without promotion into a hearing or a rule). T050/T051 and T033/T034 (green and foundling) were offstage and stand held with no seventh sighting; T004 (old life offstage, no present-tense break and no tagged memory) advanced. Nothing was joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended.

Chapter 49 is written and closed: Days 198–200, Ember 19–21, Ninthday to Firstday, Y.H. 813, a normal three-day chapter in the kiln month's third working week, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 48 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). There was no new named character with a real role; Berr Clay, Hessa Rale and Ulda were on stage on Day 199 at the Cross and market, the foundling a shape and the green a colour, and no seventh of either came. **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** Ember's second Account-day is read over the basket on Day 199. Berr Clay comes up short off the clay road with the red office cord of his office folded square under his coat and green glaze at one mouth-corner, wipes his thumb twice before touching nothing, holds a narrow slate unopened to his forearm, passes a finger over the figure and reads *One in twelve, as the notice says* low into the top of the crowd, the figure going twice over the basket, once for the moot and once for the households that come late; he says *The levy is called. It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, and goes off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he has done a kindness. The district's arithmetic for the kiln month changes for the second time and cannot be changed back; the printed figure stands unchallenged and a figure read aloud is still not the figure paid. Hessa carries the slate up the road unread, answering the question put and not the one behind it; the soft-cuffed clerk stays set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure; the bell goes four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told. Crucially the reading does **not** answer the man carried out on Day 188: no figure, collector or slate knows what he cost, no collector is called, no cause is named in Grathic and the pale line stays on its own place. Advanced, none paid: T071/T072 (198, 199 and 200 exact, six months and eighteen through twenty days, told by nobody); T088 (fire's fifth missing, payer unnamed for 198–200 days, three counts in three places); T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, nine stoppings with no tenth, the hand's inch asked on three days and held four ways on a sixth place never laid across the others); T078 (heard bell beside told, four four and a gap, nobody crossing); T058/T079 (instruction grammar, *the house*, half-pace, jar-glance without touch, half-beat at setting, gruel from the house's own dish, mark untouched, two rooms that do not touch); T015/T041/T047/T077 (second reading, figure read twice not figure paid, locked box and true subtraction, costs sent to the pot and the weather and entered nowhere); T080 (Hessa's inward slate, eighth record-first hand, advanced not paid); T090 (clerk still set for an uncalled measure); T050/T051 and T033/T034 (sixth green still a colour and foundling a shape, no seventh of either); T036/T076 (six counted and seventh at the lip, ear-sentence short of the loan, Seed 16 breath unspent, nine with no tenth, loan uncalled); T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (men unnamed before first grey, dust parted from slip, note withheld from the wrong wind, damper's double bite and remembered holding, two shut-room tellings with no third, *he's not Vail* sealed, promise ungiven); T006 (stone cold in its loft pot); T100 (charcoal unread); T060/T086/T097 (crust, two shelf gaps, pan's gone ring, field's empty place on separate trays, no third gap, no fourth likeness, no finger drawn); T009/T013/T092/T098 (yard goes wide around the unswept places and the carriers' place); T064 (pale line on its own place, no cause, no collector); T004 (old life offstage — no tag, no present-tense break). T060/T086/T097 (one crust with its unnamed empty place, two shelf gaps in a room that does not touch, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on trays that do not touch; no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause, no finger drawn); T093/T094/T096 carried (the cold foot uncured and unnamed). Nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, and no new thread opened. Foreshadowing planted and held: the twice-read figure, the twice-told burned-slate word, the eight record-first hands and the soft-cuffed clerk's inward hand stay on separate places; a row of them standing long enough would call itself an account and want a payer, and none stands. Grain 10 (Day 219) is held as the first Account-day and market of Grain, where the seventh green and the seventh sighting are owed and must not come early. Forward, not closed.

Chapter 53 is written and closed: Days 210–212, Grain 1–3, Firstday to Thirdday, a normal three-day chapter in the first working week of Grain, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand as they stood at the close of Chapter 52 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). There was no new named character with a real role, no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage; Ember went out without a boundary drawn and Grain came on with neither of the district's two month-names corrected, and there is no market and no Account-day until Grain 10 (Day 219). **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** the month turns without a boundary and the firing thins into the ordinary tending of a year begun, the count going into two hundred and ten, eleven and twelve exact, complete and unspent and spoken by nobody (T071/T072), the carried fire's fifth without a payer for the same three days (T088), the debt against a firing of tiles another tend older and still neither paid, settled nor named (T009/T013/T092), Tarn's seventh year at its very end and still not given (T036/T076), and Berr's box with Ember's third true subtraction up the clay road and not reached back (T015/T041/T047 held). **The second advancement, also unpaid:** the hand is asked six times across the three days and answered as the body answers — down before the counted breath, held for the small intake, down at once, held to the river's low interval with no count anywhere in the house, held again for the small intake, and in the small hours of Day 212 held through a whole counted breath at Sella's fire in the room that does not touch his and down with the breath — the old two-rhythm coordination remembered, filed on a sixth place beside the others and laid across nothing, not a longer reach, stronger hand, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, one of the three held changes, key, power or rule, with no payer named (T061/T082). Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (210, 211 and 212 exact, six months and thirty through thirty-two days, told by nobody); T088 (payer unnamed for all three days, the river's note and the boards' knot as the other two counts in three places that do not become one); T061/T082 (six hearings with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth with six answers, the file exact, complete and worth nothing); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*, *The house will keep it*; half-pace; jar-glance without a touch; pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed; widow's mark untouched because no morning, shelf, rack, yard or dusk was an opening — no opening in thirty, thirty-one and thirty-two days); T100 (charcoal unread, finger kept on top of the shelf's front edge); T077 (cost-questions to the pot and the weather, nothing priced, refused or entered, a number in a fist going back into a coat with nothing paid); T036/T076 (six counted and the seventh at the lip, ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, nine with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, Seed 16 breath unspent, loan uncalled, no collector on any morning); T006 (warm stone cold and unreconciled in its unmarked loft pot); T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (meal-fine grey dust parted from slip, dusty and clean forearms on one tray, bent finger held in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, men unnamed into the back room, the burned-slate word still a direction arrived nowhere beside the dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between and no third telling); T009/T013/T092/T098 (Garl kept in objects, yard going around the absences with a look and a wide step and no number, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause); T015/T041/T047, T080 and T090 held offstage in the boy's file, not on stage and not reached back. Foreshadowing held: Grain 10 (Day 219) is the first Account-day and market of Grain where the seventh green and the seventh sighting are owed and must not come early; the wrong-wind saying that a pot singing early generally sings itself out early stays a saying, costs no pot and names no cause for the man carried out on Day 188; the three held changes stay on three places and are not allowed to stand up as a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer and none has been named; no third telling of the shut counting room came, no finger was drawn across the absences and nothing was filled in to make a row come out even.

Chapter 54 is written and closed: Days 213–215, Grain 4–6, Fourthday to Sixthday, a normal three-day chapter in the first working week of Grain, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close as stood at the close of Chapter 53 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). There was no new named character with a real role, no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage; the district's two names for the months went uncorrected and there is no market until Grain 10 (Day 219). **The chapter's one meaningful change, gathered and not paid:** on Day 214 a kiln-hand come up from High Kiln on a wrong wind said the pot that sang before its time last month had not been heard from since, and the saying turned by a hair from sings itself out early to has not been heard from since; the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes a wound apart — a direction with no hand at the foot of it, no owner, no room, no cause and no figure — and set the knowing on the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them (T061/T082 advanced and unpaid, T064, T035/T031). Jorra, who knew that pot's full note three turns before it left his hands and holds the nearly-full answer with air under it and a want in it, let the saying stand as a saying, gave no name to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188 and made no lesson of the green on the wind; no pot was lost and no collector called. **A second advancement, also unpaid:** on Day 215 the burned-slate word came over the boards a third time from Slate Row, spoken over the boards and not to the basket on the same breath as the barley price and the weather, with no owner, room, hand at the flame or reason; it is a **third telling of the burned slate** and still **not a third telling of the counting room shut in the war**, which stayed at two, and the boy set it on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three, a direction arrived nowhere and nothing a clerk could chart (T035/T031/T065/T095 advanced, not paid). Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (two hundred and thirteen, fourteen and fifteen kept exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody, six months and thirty-three days to six months and thirty-five days); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*, *The house will keep it*, half-pace, jar-glance without a touch, lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, widow's mark untouched since before the kiln on Ember 1 — no opening in thirty-three, thirty-four and thirty-five days, the two rooms that do not touch); T100 (charcoal unread, finger on top of the shelf's front edge); T077 (cost-questions to the pot and the weather, nothing priced, refused or entered, a number shut in a fist paying nothing); T036/T076 (six counted and the seventh at the lip, ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped as a remembered interval, nine with no tenth, Seed 16 breath unspent, loan uncalled, no collector); T035/T031/T062 (meal-fine grey dust, dusty and clean forearms on one tray, men unnamed into the back room, the bent finger held in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, *he's not Vail* sealed, promise ungiven); T009/T013/T092/T098 (Garl in objects, the carrying arm a record in muscle that knows weight and not cost, absences gone around with no number); T006 (stone cold in its loft pot); T064 (lead glaze on a wrong wind a direction, no second edge paid); T050/T051 and T033/T034 held (no seventh green or sighting, none owed before Grain 10); T060/T086/T097 (absences on separate trays, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause); T093/T094/T096 carried. Nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended; forward, not closed.

Chapter 55 is written and closed: Days 216–218, Grain 7–9, Seventhday to Ninthday, a normal three-day chapter in the kiln month's second working week, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close as stood at the close of Chapter 54 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). There was no new named character with a real role, no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage; the district's two names for the months went uncorrected and there is no market until Grain 10 (Day 219). **The chapter's one meaningful change, gathered and not paid:** on Day 217 a kiln-hand come up from High Kiln on a wrong wind with a barrow said over the boards that there was a pot gone quiet on every row come the thaw. The singular report bent back toward the old general proverb so that three shapings stood on one tray — sings itself out early, has not been heard from since, gone quiet on every row — and the boy took them apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound, refusing to let three likenesses stand up and call themselves a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer and no payer stood to any shaping (T061/T082 near-full tray advanced and unpaid, T064, T035/T031). The sweet edge of lead glaze rode up off High Kiln on the wrong wind and went out on it, a direction and not a second edge paid; Jorra let the saying stand, no pot was lost, no cause stood up and no name was given to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188. **A second advancement, also unpaid:** on Day 218 the burned-slate word came over the boards a fourth time from Slate Row, spoken over the boards and not to the basket on the same breath as the price of barley and the weather, with no owner, room, hand at the flame or reason. It is a **fourth telling of the burned slate** and still **not a third telling of the counting room shut in the war**, which stays at two; the boy set it on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three, a direction arrived nowhere, not a cause, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment and nothing a clerk could chart (T035/T031/T065/T095 advanced, not paid). No slate burned under any roof and no new fire was lit at the kiln mouth. Advanced or carried, none paid: T071/T072 (two hundred and sixteen, seventeen and eighteen kept exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody, six months and thirty-six days to six months and thirty-eight days); T088 (the carried fire's fifth without a payer for all three days, the river's note and the boards' knot as the other two counts in three places that do not become one); T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers now holding three shapings of the pot saying advanced and unpaid, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, three held changes plus the loft scrape, the pale line and the held inch, and six answers filed on a sixth place, the last in the small hours of Day 218 held through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire in the other room, kept through the little silence after her count and down on the river's next low note — a two-rhythm coordination, remembered, not a change made again, not a longer reach, hearing, stopping, payment, key, power or rule, with no payer named); T058/T079 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*, *The house will keep it*, half-pace, jar-glance without a touch, hand shut half over nothing, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, widow's mark untouched since before the kiln on Ember 1 — no opening in thirty-six, thirty-seven and thirty-eight days, the two rooms that do not touch); T100 (charcoal unread, uncounted and uncopied, finger on top of the shelf's front edge); T077 (cost-questions at the door and the well to the pot and the weather, nothing priced, refused or entered, a number in a fist paying nothing); T036/T076 (six counted and the seventh at the lip, ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped as a remembered interval with no figure and no name, nine with no tenth, Seed 16 breath unspent, loan uncalled, no collector on Grain 7, 8 or 9, warm stone cold in its loft pot); T035/T031/T062 (meal-fine grey dust, dusty and clean forearms on one tray, three or more men unnamed into the back room, the bent finger held in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, *he's not Vail* sealed, promise ungiven); T064 (lead glaze on wrong winds a direction, no second edge paid); T009/T013/T092/T098 (Garl in objects, the carrying arm a record in muscle that knows weight and not cost, absences gone around with a look and a wide step and no number, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause); T006 (stone cold and unreconciled in its unmarked loft pot, its scrape on a fourth place); T050/T051 and T033/T034 held (no seventh green or seventh sighting, none owed before Grain 10); T060/T086/T097 (absences on separate trays, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause); T015/T041/T047, T080, T090 held offstage; T093/T094/T096 carried; T004 (old life offstage — no tag, no present-tense break). Chapter 56 is written and closed: Days 219–221, Grain 10–12, Tenthday to Secondday, a normal three-day chapter in the middle working week of Grain, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread** and closed none; one hundred threads stand at the close as stood at the close of Chapter 55 (65 planted, 34 open, 1 paid). Grain's first Account-day and market came on Day 219: Berr read *one in twelve, as the notice says* twice over the basket, called the levy and denied it with *your house*, never *I*, and went off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness; the district's arithmetic changed for Grain and cannot be changed back, the printed figure stands unchallenged and a figure read aloud is still not the figure paid, and the reading answers nothing for the man carried out on Day 188. **The chapter's one meaningful change:** T050/T051 and T033/T034 are each **paid as a sighting only** and remain unpaid in their deeper senses — a seventh green comes to a silent woman's mouth at the pot board (clay in the nail crescents, a pale line across the back of one hand, sweet lead glaze in the crease beside her lip, a cup turned a quarter and set back where it stood) and a seventh sighting of the foundling comes at the market's edge (a man's shoulders on a child's frame, breeches cut at the knee, silt pale in the wrist creases, a gap where a front tooth had been, the green sliver catching light and going dull, the head a half-beat late toward a set-down pot and stopping in the middle, no loaf, no slate and no sale). The first is a colour and the second a shape on places that do not touch: never Fenna or Ulda in the narration, never joined to an ear, a hearing, a seventh of the full note, a tenth stopping, a cause, a name or a romance, with Fenna's ear and Ulda's sale to the Waiting Room clerk still years ahead. Advanced and unpaid, none paid or closed: T071/T072 (two hundred and nineteen, twenty and twenty-one exact, nobody told), T088 (fifth unpaid for all three days), T061/T082 (three counts in three places, three shapings of the pot saying and the fourth burned-slate telling as directions arrived nowhere, the two shut-room tellings still two, nine stoppings with no tenth at the seventh year's very end, the Seed 16 breath unspent, six hand answers on the sixth place with the last a two-rhythm coordination remembered, and the file exact, complete and worth nothing), T015/T041/T047/T077, T078, T080, T090, T009/T013/T092/T098, T035/T031/T062/T065/T095, T058/T079/T100, T036/T076/T006, T064. No eighth green or shape, no seventh hearing, no tenth stopping, no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause, no opening, no collector, no pot lost and no name given to what ailed the carried man. Forward into Day 222, Grain 13, with the count unspent.

Chapter 60 is written and closed: Days 231–233, Grain 22–24, Secondday to Fourthday, a normal three-day chapter in the last tend of Grain, each day opened by its own `\daymark` and lived in full in two scenes, nothing skipped, poured into another or summarised. It opened **no new thread**, paid none and closed none; one hundred and one threads stand at the close (66 planted, 34 open, 1 paid), unchanged from the close of Chapter 59. There was no Account-day, no market, no Cross, no bell rung and no Berr, Hessa, clerk, green-mouthed woman or foundling on stage; Berr's changed arithmetic and Hessa's unread slate stood behind and were not brought early, and Grain 30 (Day 239) stood six mornings ahead. **The chapter's one movement, advanced and not paid:** T101 gains a **second asking** — over the boards of the Clay Yard on the afternoon of Day 232 a voice with no owner, no room, no hand at the kiln and no cause asks which row the pot went quiet on; the weather answers first and the price of barley after it, the voice goes off up the terrace and the row stands where it stood. The boy takes the question apart as a surgeon takes a wound apart and sets the second telling on the seventh place beside the first with a finger between the two, and fingers between it and the three shapings of the pot saying, the fourth burned-slate telling, the two shut-room tellings, the six hearings of the full note and the count. Two askings are not one witness; a witness stands somewhere and can be asked, and none does. **Not** a fourth shaping, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a fifth telling of the slate, not a cause, not a name and not a fifth witness — a seed for the far-off day a witness stands somewhere and can be asked (T101 advanced, unpaid). Advanced and not paid, none paid: T071/T072 (231, 232 and 233 exact, six months and fifty-one to fifty-three days, told by nobody), T088 (the carried fire's payer unnamed for all three days, the river's note and the boards' knot as the other two counts in three places that do not become one), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers, three pot sayings, the fourth burned-slate telling and the two shut-room tellings on their own places, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid with Grain's changed arithmetic unread, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, three held changes plus the loft scrape, the carried man's pale line and the held inch, and six answers of the hand filed on the sixth place — thirty-seventh through forty-second — the forty-second a two-rhythm coordination held across the wall on the river's next low note and remembered, not a power or a change made again), T058/T079/T100 (instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*, *The house will keep it*, half-pace, jar-glance without a touch, hand shut half over nothing, charcoal unread beneath the shelf, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, widow's mark untouched — no opening in fifty-one, fifty-two and fifty-three days, the two rooms that do not touch), T077 (dressed grain turned for damp, spread thin and gathered back, counted in glances and the carrying arm's weight, entered on no slate and given no figure; cost-questions to pot and weather, a fist's number returned to its sleeve), T009/T013/T092 and T098 (advanced and not paid, seven walked-around places entered by nobody, no joining to the dressed grain or the shelf's two gaps), T036/T076/T006 (six counted and the seventh held, knife at the very edge, Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings no tenth, ear-sentence stopped short of the calling word, no collector on Grain 22, 23 or 24, warm stone cold in its loft pot), T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 (meal-fine dust parted from slip, men unnamed in the back room, two shut-room tellings still two, burned-slate word at four, three pot sayings still sayings with no pot lost, *he's not Vail* sealed and the promise ungiven, damper's double bite as a remembered repetition), T064 and T050/T051/T033/T034 held with no eighth green or eighth shape, no name, no joining and no romance; T004 old life entirely offstage. No new scar, possession or change of voice was recorded; no bond changed hands, no payer named, no slate opened, no witness stood and nothing was ended.

**Status key:** open = planned, not yet in the text; planted = the seed is written; paid = the payoff is written.

## I. The great mysteries

| ID | Thread | Plant (planned) | Payoff (planned) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T001 | The branching red birth-flower across ribs and forearm — the fire writing itself into the hot trace | Ch 11 (Ilmen 17: Alda crossed two fingers at the red edge of the flower under the Vail roof without knowing it and covered the sign with the same unasked-for date); Ch 1 (Alda's word laid over the mark like a lid), Ch 2 (crossed over again in front of witnesses; still unexamined by anyone), Ch 3 (the red edge at his wrist seen by Alda at the market and covered with a date), Ch 4 (crossed again at the red edge of his wrist under the Vail roof and at a red mark at the hairline of the rekindled child in the death-house, both times unaware); Ch 7 (Alda made the sign again at the red edge of his wrist under the Vail roof and covered it with an unasked-for date); Ch 8 (made the sign again at the red edge of the birth-flower under the Vail roof on the Remembering, unaware, and covered it with the same unasked-for date); Ch 9 (Ilmen 5): Alda made the two-finger sign at the red edge of the birth-flower under the Vail roof, unaware, and covered it with the same unasked-for date. | Ch ~200 (crossed-out Y.H. 588 survey), then ~800 (same mark on another mender's arm) | planted |
| T002 | The woman in the grey coat at the edge of the flare light, writing without stopping | Ch 1 (death remembered; the pause matched to the birth-slate's dark hand), Ch 2 (the pause set beside the dark hand's a second time and still not joined), Ch 3 (the pause seen a third time at the Court's table, still not joined; the clerk is not she), Ch 6 (the pause seen a fourth time at the Court's table on Stone 20, still not joined; the clerk is not she); Ch 7 (the pause in the neat dark hand seen a fifth time at the market's edge on Stone 30, unjoined, with one untagged beat of orange flare light on wet snow); Ch 10 (no sixth sighting through Day 44 --- the sixth hearing that came out of a person on Ilmen 9 was a sound, not a pause in a neat hand) | Ch 35 (remembered clearly), 110 (grey fingers at the collector's table), ~910 | planted |
| T003 | The name Corin written on a slate before anyone saw the infant's face | Ch 1 (the slate shut flat on the bench), Ch 2 (still turned under, the neat hand unread, the name withheld from the mother who gave him up), Ch 3 (the neat upper line at the Court's table, set down before the face and unread), Ch 4 (a fourth neat hand --- the Drawing collector's --- with no pause in it at all, set beside the three and not joined to them), Ch 6 (a fourth sighting of the pause in the neat dark hand at the Court's table on Stone 20, still unread and unjoined); Ch 7 (fifth sighting of the neat upper line and its small stop at the Court's table at the market's edge, still unread and unjoined) | Ch ~610 (Marra's unfinished apology, finished by Dan) | planted |
| T004 | The lit phone under the sheet with a name he will not look at; the unsent call to Nina | Ch 1 (the light he refuses, held against the flare light), Ch 3 (the hand goes to the place where a pocket should have been and finds wool and a barrel stave; the name is still not looked at), Ch 4 (twice more --- at the Vail threshold and in the death-house --- the hand goes to the pocket-place, finds wool, and does not look at the name), Ch 5 (at the Fishstairs a collector's soft cuff crossing the bridge brings two present-tense sentences of a hospital cafeteria at four in the morning and a wrapper eaten to the last crumb; the hand goes to a pocket-place that holds wool and the lit name is still not looked at), Ch 6 (reached twice --- at the yard well over a turned chart and at the Cross under the flare --- each time finding wool, woodsmoke and river wind, the name unlooked-at); Ch 7 (reached again under the flare light at the market's edge, the hand finding wool where a lit rectangle with a name had lain, the name unlooked-at); Ch 9 (Ilmen 8): the shape of Dessa's hand on the basket handle reached in past tense, untagged, toward a coat pocket and a small cold rectangle with a name not looked at; the boat came back nine promises old on cold water and nothing crossed); Ch 10 (Ilmen 9, the held detail paid in past tense and untagged --- a hand in a grey sleeve into a coat pocket for a small cold rectangle with a name unlooked-at, a boat nine promises old on cold water unsaid, the hand coming back with wool, nothing crossing into the basket) | Ch ~1500 (the mechanism, and the decision to carry) | planted |
| T005 | Why the return was whole: deduction entered as zero, forty unspent measures, purchaser unnamed | Ch 1 (seed in the neat dark hand), Ch 2 (the hand still unread, its pause unjoined), Ch 3 (the same stop a third time; the boxes and rolls still unshown), Ch 4 (the collector's measure written in a neat ordinary hand with no stop in it --- the absence of the mark noted and filed), Ch 6 (the neat upper line and its small stop seen a fourth time at the Court's table, still unread and unjoined); Ch 7 (fifth sighting of the stop in the neat dark hand, still unread; one in twelve read again and the true deduction unspoken); Ch 8 (a whole return read aloud in a clean voice for a person not in the room, and one in twelve still printed and unchallenged at the Opening of the Slates while the true deduction stayed unspoken) | Ch ~610 and ~1450 (Veno writes the name on wet salt-paper) | planted |
| T006 | The warm stone pressed to a newborn's belly, already going cold — the hinge of both lives | Ch 1 (warmed at the Vail hearth, spent by morning, felt by nobody but him), Ch 2 (fully spent and left in the fold of grey wool on the abandoned bed in House Silt; its warmth paid twice from the Vail hearth and entered nowhere) | Ch ~330 (Sella's second roll — the stone was Garl's) and throughout | planted |

| T098 | The hand that does not come back — one hand that went into the worked strips on Seed 15 did not come out with the last hands on Seed 27, and the yard goes around the empty place in the returning line with a look and no number | Ch 26 (Seed 27, Day 116): planted at the worked strips east of Low Kiln, where the planting's last hands come out with the seed up and worked around, counted in glances and handfuls and never in a figure --- a woman lifting two fingers with chaff in the creases, a man with a barrow shaping the number of backs with his mouth and thinking better of it. One hand that went in on Seed 15 does not come back with the rest; the yard goes around its place with a look and no number, and no name, cause, accident, body, identification of which hand or payment is given. Carried through Seed 28–30, including Account-day, where the figure read aloud does not know the hand that did not come back; not laid beside the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its empty place, or the word forty. **Ch 60 (Grain 22–24, Days 231–233): advanced and not paid** — carried through the last tend of Grain with the yard going around the returning-line place at every carrying, a look and a wide step and no number; the dressed grain lay covered and unfigured beside it and the boy drew no finger between the two keepings. Still no name, cause, body, figure or payer. | Ch ~1500 (a later body found, or a figure at last given for what the month cost, read with the district's complete private account); held as a subtraction entered in no slate beside the gone crust, the gone stone and the coal the gull took | planted |

| T099 | The reed-cutter's cord — a tally kept in wool or reed-fibre where no Court thumb will read it, a third private record in the district | Ch 27 (Reed 3, Day 122): at the Weir a reed-cutter came along the plank walk with a bundle on his shoulder and a short length of grey cord looped inside his coat; he set the bundle on the blessing stone, straightened the cord on his thumb, turned it so the boy saw the knots standing at arm's length, tied one low with his teeth and a pull of the thumb, put it back inside his coat and went to lift another trap. To the weir woman who could not read a cord he said *You can ask the cord* and *No more can the Court*. | T100 | The tally under the shelf — a charcoal keeping on the UNDERSIDE of the Vail loaf shelf, a private count put where nobody thinks to look | Ch 30 (Reed 10, Day 129, Scene 2): from the basket's low angle at the Vail kiln-house the boy saw short strokes in charcoal on the underside of the loaf shelf, grouped in little clusters a finger's breadth apart, some dark and pressed hard enough to leave a smooth track where the stick dug, some the colour of old smoke and gone nearly into the grain, fainter at the row's end as if the hand had gone away from the task or grown tired of pressing. They did not make Grathic words and no slate had been set under them to catch what they meant. He did not read, count, copy or show them, did not lay them beside the two gaps above, the fold's one crust, Alda Reed's tin, Mira Silt's cords, Orra Weir's knot-string, Dessa Silt's pewter tin or the reed-cutter's cord, and set the marks on a place of their own, advanced and unpaid, held for the far-off day a hand not his lays the district's private records together. On Reed 12 (Day 131, Scene 7) Sella Vail reached a finger BENEATH the board without looking at it and without a word and brought it away; he saw the hand go under and come back and saw nothing of what the marks said, and made no cause of the habit. No figure, no stroke counted, no copying hand, no joining to the two gaps above or to any other absence. | Ch ~1400 (laid beside Mira's two cords, Orra Weir's knot-string, Alda Reed's six pages in the little tin, Dessa Silt's pewter tin above the abandoned bed and the reed-cutter's cord as the district's complete private account, read with the figure at last given for what the reed month cost); held now with no figure, no payer and no reader, and never read or joined in Chapters 31–33 | planted |

| T101 | The question about the row — a voice with no owner asks over the boards which row a pot went quiet on, and the weather and the price of barley answer instead | Ch 59 (Grain 21, Day 230, Scene 6): over the boards of the Clay Yard, in the afternoon, a voice with no room, no hand at the kiln and no cause asks low which row the pot went quiet on; the weather answers first and the price of barley after it, and the voice goes off up the terrace while the row stands where it stood. The boy takes the question apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound — what it asks, where it stands, what it would want before any man could answer it, and who would have to stand under the answer to pay for it — and files it on a **seventh place of its own** with a finger between it and the three shapings of the pot saying, the fourth telling of the burned slate, the two shut-room tellings, the six hearings of the full note and the count. It is **not** a fourth shaping of the pot saying (a proverb and a question are different instruments), not a hearing, not a seventh of the full note, not a cause, not a name and not a fifth telling of the slate. A row is a place; a place wants a witness; a witness wants a day when somebody stands somewhere and can be asked. No witness stands yet. **Ch 60 (Grain 23, Day 232): advanced and not paid** — the question came back over the boards a **second** time in the same voice with no owner, no room, no hand at the kiln and no cause; the weather answered first and the price of barley after it, the voice went off up the terrace and the row stood where it stood. The boy set the second telling on the seventh place beside the first with a finger between the two and fingers between them and the three pot sayings, the fourth burned-slate telling, the two shut-room tellings, the six hearings and the count. Two askings are not one witness; a witness stands somewhere and can be asked, and none does. Still **not** a fourth shaping, a hearing, a seventh of the full note, a fifth telling of the slate, a cause, a name or a fifth witness — a seed for the far-off day a witness stands somewhere and can be asked. | Far off, when a witness stands somewhere and can be asked (no owner, room, hand at the kiln, figure or cause is given now); held on its own place beside the other directions arrived nowhere | planted |

## III. Grudges, betrayals and promises

| ID | Thread | Plant (planned) | Payoff (planned) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
