*90 threads. Forty-eight stand planted at the close of Chapter 9 --- Chapter 9 opened no new thread, closed none and paid none; it advanced T001, T004, T007-T009, T013, T015, T023, T025, T031, T035, T036, T041, T044, T047, T058-T061, T065, T071-T073 and T076-T088, chiefly T082 (the nearly-full pot as the interval's first earthly answer, advanced and not paid), T083/T085 (honeyed ice, the opened hairline, the Singing Sill early, the high clay-heavy brook) and T086 (two crusts, no third). The pause in the neat dark hand has now been seen five times --- the flare, the birth-slate, the Cross on Stone 10, the Court's table on Stone 20 and the market's edge on Stone 30 --- and the fifth did not join the first four; the Drawing collector's hand still has no pause and stays in its own place. The flat note has five hearings from four sources and no sixth; Stone 13, Stone 17 and Stone 25 added three intervals, kept on a separate tray from the hearings, and Ilmen 6 added a nearly-full answer to the Stone 13 interval, filed on its own tray and not counted as a sixth. Day 38 will add the sixth hearing and the second from a person and will pay the hand-on-the-basket detail held from Ilmen 8; neither has been written. Two crusts lie side by side in the fold, one spent and one given, neither entered nor eaten. Five rooms --- birth room, kiln-house, death-house, Court's room and the neighbour's room two doors down --- hold the same bench, pot, draught and gap, and the boy has kept them apart on purpose. Low Kiln still reads one in twelve and gives out three, then two, then whatever the moot has already decided; the word forty has not been spoken. A passage changes the count and nothing else: the fifth sighting buys no word, no hand and no standing, and a week of keeping the count exactly buys a thumb on wicker the boy cannot spend.*

*Chapter 10 closes on Day 44, Ilmen 15, Y.H. 813, with the boy forty-four days old and nothing joined, paid or explained except a sound, a shape and a word. It opened no fifth pause and no seventh hearing; it paid T087's Day 38 payoff (the sixth hearing, the second from a person, out of a woman in the brook) and the hand-on-wicker detail held since Ilmen 8, advanced T004/T008/T044/T061/T073 and the first edge of T009's heresy as an omission, planted T089 (the pewter tin above the bed) and T090 (the clerk at the Fishstairs for an uncalled measure), and moved T024 from open to planted when Orra tied the forty-first knot and said the word into the basket. The count stands at six hearings from four sources plus one from a person, five unjoined pauses, three intervals, the told bell beside the heard bell, the nearly-full answer on its own tray, and four, four and a gap. Two crusts still lie in the fold and one will go missing on Day 45, with nobody saying who took it. Dessa is dead and stays dead; no collector was called and nobody named the omission. Fifty stand planted at the close of Chapter 10 (verified against the table: 50 planted, 39 open, 1 paid); T087 was paid, no thread was ended outright.

*Chapter 11 closes on Day 47, Ilmen 18, Y.H. 813, with the boy forty-seven days old and nothing joined, paid or explained except a shape that can now be read and a taking nobody will name. It opened T091 (the three hands on a tray) and moved T083 from open to planted; T086 paid its first edge on Day 46 --- the crust is gone, eaten by nobody, lifted in no one's sight and spoken of by nobody --- while its deeper payoff stays for ~1500. T001, T007–T009, T013, T023–T025, T035, T036, T041, T044, T047, T058–T061, T065, T071–T073, T076–T080, T082, T084–T086, T088–T090 were advanced; chiefly T024 (the knot's shape legible without its name, the word unspoken, the watching continued), T088 (the coal gone up with a bird, entered nowhere), T086 (the crust gone), T085/T083 (frost-bloom, ice going soft, higher brook, early low note, weak board heard-of), T090 (the clerk still set) and T071/T072 (the exact count carried). The six hearings stayed six from four sources plus one from a person, no seventh; the five pauses, the nearly-full note, the told bell, the absence shaped like a collection, the word and the knot, and the two women's cords all stayed on separate trays and unjoined. Ninety-one threads stand at the close of Chapter 11 (52 planted, 38 open, 1 paid); nothing was ended outright.**

*Chapter 12 is written and closed: Days 48–54, Ilmen 19–25, Y.H. 813, a seven-day passage chapter lived one dated entry at a time. It opened no new thread, paid none and closed none; it advanced T007–T009, T013, T015, T023–T025, T035, T036, T041, T047, T058–T061, T065, T071–T073, T076–T086, T089–T091 — chiefly T085 (frost-bloom written back, the oiled paper slack in its frame, a silence where an argument stood, more hairlines stopping short of the edge, the Singing Sill early, the high clay-heavy brook, all called weather), T083 (the weak plank walk board said plainly still there and trodden by nobody), T082 (a SECOND nearly-full answer to the Stone 13 interval, advanced and not paid), T077 (one in twelve printed and unchallenged over the basket, the thaw number from fist to mouth to shoulder and given to nobody), T023 (a fifth hidden note and the tin touched once unopened) and T086/T091 (one crust and an empty place carried seven days, the three hands still on their tray). The six hearings stayed six, five from four sources and one from a person, beside and never among; no seventh came, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no joining of word, shape, cords, pauses or trays, and no accident made of the board. Ninety-one threads stand at the close of Chapter 12, unchanged from Chapter 11 (52 planted, 38 open, 1 paid); nothing was ended outright.

*Chapter 13 is written and closed: Days 55–60, Ilmen 26–30 and Silt 1, Y.H. 813, a six-day passage lived one dated entry at a time. It opened T092 (the new hairline of grey in the Vail corner, called thaw and not swept, toward the flood of Ch 14–15 and the later day a house is left where it is and still kept) and left T065 standing planted while planting its long-promised seed in the text: the Greybank saying of a counting room shut in the last war with the damper closed and men dead of the breath, told at the well as history eighteen leagues east, filed on its own tray beside the told bell, the heard bell and the kiln's gap with no cause written across it (T065, T078, T071/T072). No thread was paid and none was ended. Advanced: T001 and T007 (Alda's unaware sign at the flower's red edge, covered with the date she gave Dessa); T008 and T073 (*warm and breathing* exact and not true, curdled through to a death twenty-two mornings uncollected, name and third day still unsaid); T023 (tin touched once in the bodice and no sixth note written --- the hidden record stays at five and the sixth page waits for Day 64); T089 and T074 (pewter tin above the empty bed seen again with its pale fold and thumb-bright lid, neither tin opened); T081 (House Silt entered as a room left where it is); T071/T072 and T077 (exact three days to Silt kept by the boy against the house's loaves, glances and an uncalled space at the shelf's end; the number for the thaw from fist to mouth to shoulder and given to nobody); T035 (bone-coloured slip, no grey past the bent finger, damper ahead of Sella's instruction, back room unsaid, broken birth-slates under the rack, *he's not Vail* sealed); T036 and T076 (knife on its sill with six counted and the seventh held, broom over the collector's road without turning, head not turned at a vessel's note, two ears under one roof, loan uncalled, late thaw wanted because it puts off the paying); T009 and T013 (salt coat, hollow seat, clean dark line and death-house ridge measured by the same thumb, barrow of grey out unread, proverb said to a pot with no crack, prepared sweeping already done, uncalled tile debt); T083 (weak board at the Weir still there and trodden by nobody); T090 and T080 (Fishstairs clerk still set, glimpsed hand to slate before eyes, silent, filed apart from Marra, the Drawing collector and the Court-table clerk); T015, T041 and T047 (one in twelve read twice over the basket, thumb wiped twice, speaker removed with *there'll be a slate at the watch*, box still locked); T059, T025, T024 and T084 (two cords with the second twisted twice beneath, knots uncounted, Nine Pots unvisited, board beside Orra's not touching, no crust, Mira's not-looking a keeping where Berr's is a pricing); T078 and T082 (told bell beside heard bell, early Singing Sill note and kiln's gap kept apart, two nearly-full answers advanced and not paid); T085 (river stirring under the ice, new grey and higher brook called thaw and weather); T060 and T086 (one crust stiff in the fold and the empty place carried into Silt without a name); T061 (six hearings beside and never among, no seventh in the new month); T058 (instruction to fire, wool and bucket and never to the basket, half-pace from every open slate, *the house*, widow's mark before an opening only); T079 (mark touched before the kiln was opened and not for a grey line, a basket or a slate); T088 (coal the brine-gull took fifteen days gone, entered in no slate and missed in no account). The old life stayed offstage: no present-tense Chicago break and no tagged remembered line. Ninety-two threads stand at the close of Chapter 13 (53 planted, 38 open, 1 paid); nothing was paid, closed or joined, and the count crosses into Silt 2 unspent.**

*Chapter 15 closes on Day 74, Silt 15, Y.H. 813, with the boy seventy-four days old and nothing joined, paid or explained except a sound filed as a shape and a heresy nobody spoke. It opened T093 and left T023 unspent --- the sixth page written on Day 64 was carried, not repaid again. Every other thread was advanced and nothing was closed: chiefly T083/T085/T092 (the flood up and down, the gone board, the honeyed ice and early note renamed past tense, the new grey unswept), T061/T082 (the district's note filed as a fifth low thing beside four, no seventh, three near-full answers), T009/T013 (the water going around the lines and the plain heresy that a thing left where it lies is kept), T015/T041/T047/T077 (one in twelve over standing water, the true subtraction unspoken, the number arriving nowhere), T058/T079 (the half-pace, every instruction to the fire, wool and bucket, the mark untouched in seven days), T059/T025/T024 (cords and knot-string unread, board beside board not touching), T089/T074/T023 (tin, hollow and turned page shut), T035/T031/T036/T076 (dust on Silt 14, broken slates under the rack, the knife and the seventh year, two ears and two silences). The six hearings stayed six; the word was not spoken, no knot counted, no tin opened, no name taken from Dessa, no accident made of the board. Ninety-three threads stand at the close (54 planted, 38 open, 1 paid). Forward, not closed.*

| T077 | Number for the flood went from fist to mouth to shoulder and reached nobody; a woman who wanted a figure was told to ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. |
| T079 | Widow's mark not touched once in three days, because no opening came; a well, a slate set out to dry, a basket and a bed are not openings. |
| T080 | Soft-cuffed Fishstairs clerk still set for a measure nobody called, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, reported again on Silt 16 and 18; filed apart from Marra, the Drawing collector and the Court-table clerk; no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand. |

| T082 | Three near-full answers stand advanced and not paid, no fourth; three intervals stay objects; the word stays a saying with the readable double turn beside it and the weight of a thumb apart from both; the half-beat lies with the intervals but is not a hearing. |
| T083 | Steps came back four, four and a landing a hand's breadth out of line that a whole market walked over carefully without making a fall of it; weak board gone or trodden flat under the silt and made into no accident. |
| T084 | Mira's not-speaking, her not-looking and Berr's taking-himself-out-of-his-sentence kept as three omissions of one shape that do not touch; Sella did not look at the foundling, the sliver or the loaf. |
| T085 | Honeyed ice, the hairlines that stopped short of the edge and the early Singing Sill note spoken of as the thaw that was; the new grey called thaw and not swept. |
| T086 | One crust and its unnamed empty place carried through three days, no third; Sella's, Alda's and Mira's hands stay on their tray and do not become a cause. |
| T088 | Carried fire under blind ash and the back of a broken pot, warming the boards by degrees and missing one breath in every four, its payer unnamed. |
| T089 | Pewter tin above the empty bed and Alda's sixth page stand unread in two materials, carried to no door. |
| T090 | Soft-cuffed clerk still set at the Fishstairs with a slate for a measure nobody called, hand to slate before eyes, still set on Silt 16 and reported still set on Silt 18. |
| T091 | Sella's, Alda's and Mira's hands stay on their tray as possible takers of the crust and are not turned into a cause. |
| T092 | New finer grey beside the old clean dark line unswept through three days and called thaw; the two lines kept in two places and neither became the other. |
| T093 | Cold foot below the sill still the cold to the woman who wrapped it and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused, unnamed, carried past the recovery without a hand to place. |

*Chapter 16 closes on Day 77, Silt 18, Y.H. 813, with the boy seventy-seven days old and nothing joined, paid, explained or ended. It opened no new thread: the watcher's warm loaf (T033) and his half-beat delay when a head turns toward a pot (T034) moved from **open** to **planted**, the shape and the tell waiting for their betrayal years hence while no secret has changed hands that a slate could read. Every other thread was advanced and none was paid or closed, chiefly T071/T072/T077 (the count exact at seventy-five, seventy-six, seventy-seven against loaves, glances and a space nobody calls a number), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among, no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, the half-beat filed as after the knock and not a hearing), T058/T057/T079 (half-pace, instruction to the fire, wool, bucket and broom, never the basket, the mark untouched in three days), T025/T059/T024/T089/T074/T023 (both cords, the knot-string, the tin above the bed and the sixth page all unread, T023 not paid again), T035/T031/T036/T076 (grey dust and the unnamed men, broken slates under the rack, the seventh year nearly gone and held, two ears and two silences), T007/T008/T073 (the unaware sign, the date over it, the mercy-lie curdled against a woman thirty-nine mornings dead), T009/T013/T092/T085 (the mud swept around, both lines unswept and called thaw), T086/T060/T091 (one crust, an unnamed empty place, three hands on their tray), T083 (the steps back out of line and walked over carefully), T080/T090 (the clerk still set) and T093 (the cold foot still unnamed). T033 and T034 were planted, not paid; the sale is still a shape and no betrayal has landed. Ninety-three threads stand at the close (56 planted, 36 open, 1 paid). Forward, not closed.*

The six hearings stayed six, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted, no tin or box opened, no name taken from Dessa, no accident made of the board or the step, and no joining of the two tellings to any kiln. | T060 | Gruel, warmth from the carried fire, the crust and the fold all given and entered nowhere; each warmth has a payer who does not stand in the room to be thanked; kindness done as maintenance and not named. |
| T061 | Six hearings from four sources plus the one from a person stayed six beside and never among on all three days; no seventh hearing from vessels' notes on Days 91 and 92; no Grathic word for the full note. |
| T065 | Two tellings of the shut counting room stayed on their own tray joined to no kiln, likeness and difference kept (breath through the nose against breath in the throat, both saying *not the war's hand*); Sella told the fire it was the thaw. |
| T071 | Count kept exactly at ninety-one, ninety-two and ninety-three against the house's arithmetic in loaves, glances and a space at the row's end nobody names; a fist of seed and a look counted nothing aloud. |
| T072 | Private file gains the strips as a direction become worked ground, the damper read as two mercies in one bite, the twice-wiped thumb and the two empty places in two rooms --- each on its own tray, none joined and none entered. |
| T073 | Name and number still unsaid by Dessa, dead and uncollected; Alda's exact date stands over the sign where no living person can take it off. |
| T074 | Grey wool below the pewter tin holds only the hollow where the small pale river stone lay; nobody says it is gone. |
| T076 | Two ears and two silences kept their equality under one roof; Tarn's head did not turn at vessels' notes on Days 91 and 92 and he did not name them; the loan that can hear remained uncalled. |
| T077 | Figure read aloud not figure paid; figure goes into a slate and not a strip; number for the planting reaches nobody and the fist goes back to its owner with the number still in it. |
| T079 | Widow's mark touched at no hour in three days --- not at a strip, broom, well, market, door or bed, because none was an opening; she has lost a husband to that danger and never describes it that way. |
| T080 | Soft-cuffed Fishstairs clerk still set with his slate to his forearm for a measure nobody called, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, filed apart from Marra, the Drawing collector and the Court-table clerk; no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand. |
| T081 | House Silt entered again on Seed 4 as the fifth likeness of the room with bench, fold, tin and gap, held beside the four others and not joined to them. |
| T082 | Three near-full answers stand advanced and not paid, three intervals stay on another tray, four low things stand in four places with the river's note a fifth and word forty unspoken; the damper's double bite filed as an anatomy worth nothing. |
| T083 | Steps come back four, four and a landing a hand's breadth out of line that a market walks carefully over without a fall; the weak board at the Weir is gone or trodden flat and nobody has gone down to see which; stopping hairlines kept as three gaps. |
| T084 | Mira's not-looking, a keeping, set beside Berr's not-looking at the strips, a pricing, in places that do not touch; Alda's slate before the child and Sella's fold righted without a look kept apart on the same tray. |
| T086 | One brown-edged crust lies stiff in the fold with the empty place beside it unnamed and no third; the place at the end of the loaf row and the place at his ear lie in two rooms and do not touch. |
| T088 | Carried fire at four, four and a gap under blind ash and the back of a broken pot, its payer unnamed for ninety-three days; the coal the gull took entered in no slate and missed in none. |
| T089 | Round pewter tin above the abandoned board-bed keeps its pale fold and thumb-bright rub unopened; two private records in two materials stand apart in one room, read by nobody and carried to no door. |
| T090 | Clerk still set at the Fishstairs for a measure nobody called; hand to slate before eyes to stalls as the market walks over the out-of-line landing. |
| T092 | New finer grey unswept beside the old clean dark line and called the thaw; direction become worked ground with seed in it and still no figure spoken. |
| T093 | Child's cold foot below the sill still the cold to one woman and weather to the yard on all three days, uncured, uncaused, unnamed; the surgeon's reading has the usefulness of an instrument behind glass. |
| T094 | The staying cold carried into the first planting week as interest gathering on the wound T093 planted, still with no hand placed and no cause written. |
| T095 | No third telling came in the first planting week; the two tellings stay on their tray joined to no kiln, likeness and difference kept. |
| T096 | The silt-leach itself still held unnamed, uncaused and uncured under the standing cold; the first planting week carries the seed of Arc 3 forward without paying it. |

*Chapter 19 closes on Day 93, Seed 4, Fourthday, Y.H. 813, with the boy ninety-three days old, three months, and nothing joined, paid, explained or ended. It opened no new thread and paid none; the tally stands at ninety-six (59 planted, 36 open, 1 paid). Every thread above was advanced and none was closed, chiefly T071/T072 (the exact count ninety-one through ninety-three against loaves, glances and the unnamed space, with a direction at last become worked ground and still unfigured), T077/T041/T047 (figure read aloud not figure paid, into a slate and not a strip, the twice-wiped thumb, the locked box and the true subtraction going over the strips unspoken), T058/T079 (instruction to the fire, wool, bucket and broom and never to the basket, *the house has said it*, the mark untouched at no opening in three days), T035/T031 (grey dust past the bent finger, men unnamed in the back room, broken birth-slates under the rack, the damper ahead of the sentence and the bite that holds and takes with no word for both), T036/T076 (six counted and the seventh held, *Six counted. The next I don't count*, the collector's road unswept along, two ears and two silences, the loan uncalled), T009/T013/T092 (both lines unswept and called thaw, a direction turned ground), T060/T086 (one crust, the unnamed empty place, the two absences in two rooms), T088 (carried fire with its payer unnamed for ninety-three days and the gull's coal entered nowhere), T061/T082 (six hearings beside and never among, no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals, four low sounds with the river's note a fifth), T065/T095 (two tellings of the shut room kept on their own tray), T059/T025/T024 (both cords, uncounted knots, the thin child's knot untouched, boards not touching), T090/T080 (the soft-cuffed clerk still set, hands kept apart, no sixth sighting), T083 (out-of-line landing and the gone board at the Weir), T007/T008/T073/T001 (Alda's unaware sign, the date over it, the mercy-lie curdled against a death nobody alive can correct), T023/T089/T074 (both private records in two materials shut, **T023 not paid again**, no seventh page) and T093/T094/T096 (the staying cold carried into planting, the silt-leach still unnamed and held for Arc 3). No seventh hearing came, no knot was counted, no tin or box was opened, no name or number came from Dessa, no accident was made of the step or the board, and the two tellings joined to no kiln. Forward, not closed.*

*Chapter 20 closes on Day 100, Seed 11, Firstday, Y.H. 813, with the boy one hundred days old, three months and ten days, and nothing joined, paid, explained or ended. No seventh hearing came, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand, no knot counted, no word forty spoken, no tin or box opened, no silt-leach, no recognition of the mother, no name or number from Dessa, no Marra in Sill, no second sale, no experiment, clamp or rank change. Ninety-six threads stand at the close (60 planted, 35 open, 1 paid). Forward, not closed.*

Six answers across six scenes, the **thirty-seventh** down at once under conditions that had sometimes answered, the **thirty-eighth** held to the small intake, the **thirty-ninth** down before the counted breath, the **fortieth** kept to the river's own low interval and down on its next low note, the **forty-first** down before the counted breath had finished, and the **forty-second** an inch carried through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of Sella's breaths in the room that does not touch his, kept through the little silence after her count and down on the river's next low note — two rhythms held across the wall, remembered, not a change made again and not a longer reach, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule. Forty-two answers since Day 213, filed on the sixth place as objects and not powers. |
| T064 | A wrong wind off High Kiln brought the sweet edge of lead glaze over the boards on Grain 23 and 24, touched the fold and went out on the wind that brought it — a direction and not a second edge paid; the green at a mouth stayed joined to no ear and no name was given to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188. |
| T071/T072 | Two hundred and thirty-one, thirty-two and thirty-three kept exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody in Sarn, Sill or Grath — six months and fifty-one days to six months and fifty-three — with nobody under any roof giving a figure for the covered grain or the hand that did not come back. |
| T077 | The dressed grain was turned for damp under its wool, spread thin to finish and gathered back by the handful near the hearth — changed in state twice more, counted in glances, handfuls and the carrying arm's weight that knows weight and not cost, entered on no slate and given no figure. Cost-questions at the threshold and the well went to the pot and the weather, and a man's number came up out of a fist toward the well and went back into his sleeve so the figure arrived nowhere. |
| T088 | The carried fire gave four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor, its payer unnamed for two hundred and thirty-one, thirty-two and thirty-three days; the river's note through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that do not become one, for agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any. |
| T093/T094/T096 | Carried, not paid: the cold foot stays the cold to the woman that wrapped it and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused, unnamed and unrecognised through the last tend of Grain. |
| T098 | **Advanced and not paid:** the yard goes around the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number on all three days; no name, cause, body, figure or payer, and not laid beside the covered grain, the shelf's two gaps, the fold's crust and gap, the pan's gone ring or the field's empty place. |
| T101 | **Advanced and not paid:** the question about the row came over the boards a **second** time on Grain 23 in a voice with no owner, room, hand at the kiln or cause; the weather answered first, the price of barley after it, the voice went off up the terrace and the row stood where it stood. Two askings are not one witness, for a witness stands somewhere and can be asked and none does; the second telling sits on the seventh place beside the first with a finger between them and fingers between them and the sayings, the slate, the shut-room tellings, the hearings and the count. Still not a fourth shaping, a hearing, a seventh, a fifth telling, a cause, a name or a fifth witness. |

It advances T071/T072, T088, T061/T082 (the thirty-seventh through the forty-second answers, the last a remembered two-rhythm coordination), T058/T079, T100, T077 and T098, and holds every other thread at its old count. | T071/T072 | Two hundred and forty, forty-one and forty-two kept exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody in Sarn, Sill or Grath — six months and sixty days to six months and sixty-two — with nobody under any roof giving a figure for the covered grain or the hand that did not come back. |
| T077 | The dressed grain was turned under its wool for damp on Smoke 2 and 3, spread thin to finish, gathered back by the handful and the fold set flat, changed in state and gaining no figure, counted in glances, handfuls and the carrying arm's weight that knows weight and not cost, entered on no slate and laid across none of the absences. Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather on Smoke 2 and 3 with nothing priced, refused or entered; none came on Smoke 1. |
| T088 | The carried fire gave four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor, its payer unnamed for two hundred and forty, forty-one and forty-two days; on Smoke 1 Sella tipped the carried coal under the back of a broken pot and the fire took, still missing its fifth. The river's note through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that do not become one, for agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any. |
| T093/T094/T096 | Carried, not paid: the cold foot stayed the cold to the woman that wrapped it and weather to the yard, uncured, uncaused, unnamed and unrecognised through Smoke 1–3. |
| T098 | Carried and not paid: the dressed grain stayed covered under wool and the yard went around the seven walked-around places, including the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number; no name, cause, body, figure or payer, and not laid beside the covered grain, the shelf's two gaps, the fold's crust and gap, the pan's gone ring or the field's empty place. |

*Chapter 63 advances T058/T079 with the first opening of Smoke, not paid, and T088 with its unnamed payer for three more days; the rule that the hand goes to the mark before the kiln and at no other hour held exactly, as on Tallow 1 and Ember 1, with no lesson, figure, slate, bond or payer made of the opening. It advances T061/T082 with the fifty-fifth through the sixtieth answers, the sixtieth a remembered two-rhythm coordination, T071/T072, T077, T064, T009/T013/T092, T100, T036/T076, T006, T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 and T004, and carries T015/T041/T047, T098, T101, T093/T094/T096 and the two sighting threads at their old counts. No new thread is opened, none is paid or closed and no count is changed. Statuses stand at the close of Chapter 63: one hundred and one threads (66 planted, 34 open, 1 paid), nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, and the count crosses into Smoke 4 unspent. Forward, not closed.*

### Chapter 64 additions to the ledger (one line per thread advanced)

| ID | Smoke 4–6 (Days 243–245) |
|---|---|
| T004 | The old life stayed entirely offstage through Smoke 4–6: no present-tense Chicago break and no `\memory{}` tag. The surgeon's register turned out the file as instruments on a towel and found it exact, complete and worth nothing, took the body's inventory in the order crust, gap, thread, cold and afterdrop, read the notched birch bark as an order of reaching and not a figure, read the fist's number as a chart turned face-down, and read the sweet edge of glaze as a direction arrived nowhere — and entered six more answers on the sixth place without promoting any of them into a reach, a power or a rule. |
| T006 | The warm stone lies cold and unreconciled in its unmarked pot in the Debt Loft on all three days, a keeping taken out and looked at once on Ember 4 and put back, which is not forgiving; its place reaches the boy only as a sound with a direction from a room he cannot enter. |
| T009/T013/T092 | Garl is kept in objects and not in a story: salt-stiff coat with the left-shoulder crease, hollow wheel-seat with its twenty-year lean, clean dark line and finer grey called the thaw unswept, proverb gone years ago to a pot with no crack, tile debt circling without a name. His name stayed unspoken two hundred and forty-three, forty-four and forty-five days; no collector came up or went down the clay road on Smoke 4, 5 or 6, and the seven walked-around places went around with a look and a wide step and no number. |
| T015/T041/T047 | **Held, not paid:** a man come down the clay road on Smoke 5 and again on Smoke 6 brought a number up out of a fist toward the yard well, looked at it and put it back into his sleeve, so the figure arrived nowhere — not a missing figure but a figure kept where no thumb could read it, and a sleeve is not a slate. Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered; the printed one in twelve stood unchallenged and the true subtraction stayed locked in Berr's box up the road, answering nothing for the man carried out on Day 188 or the hand that went in on Seed 15. |
| T035/T031/T062/T065/T095 | Three or more men unnamed went low before first grey into the finger's-width-open back room on all three mornings and got no name and no third telling; Jorra's meal-fine grey dust went past the bent finger and under the nails, dusty and clean forearms on one tray, the full note known three turns ahead and withheld on the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them; his bent finger stood in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, took the cold out, held until the end arrived at the pot, moved a hair's breadth and was left standing — a remembered repetition joined to no kiln and paired with no other fraction. The burned-slate word stayed at four, the shut-room tellings at two and the three pot sayings at three; *he's not Vail* sealed, the promise of the foundling's name neither given nor withdrawn. |
| T036/T076 | Six counted in the worn thumb-mark where the house can check and the seventh at the lip where it cannot, the knife at the very edge of its sill with Garl's unforged collector sentence beside it, the Seed 16 breath drawn at the top of each reach and not spent, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, the ear-sentence brought to the calling word on all three days as a remembered stopping with no figure and no name, the loan uncalled and no collector in the road; the stroke went over the bright patch and the collector's road without the half-check or a turn, so the Day 171 movement stays a held change beside the Day 161 fraction and the Day 166 beat. |
| T058/T079 | The widow's mark hung where it hung, touched once before the kiln on Smoke 1 and at no shelf, well, rack, road, basket, afternoon or dusk on Smoke 4, 5 or 6, because the hand goes to the mark before the kiln and at no other hour; the one opening lay behind the household and was not looked at again as a sign, lesson, mending or payment and no payer was named to it. Instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*, *The house will keep it*, half-pace, jar-glance without a touch, hand shut half over nothing, lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed. |
| T060/T086 | One stiff leathery brown-edged barley crust lay two inches from the ear with its empty place unnamed and no third; the thread was frozen in the dry heat and unwiped, and cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist-joint with the afterdrop waiting, while the pan's gone ring, the shelf's two gaps, the field's empty place and the returning-line place stayed on places that do not touch. No fourth likeness was made a cause and no finger drew the line between them. |
| T061/T082 | Six hearings beside and never among with no seventh and none invented to fill the empty place, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, three shapings of the pot saying, the fourth burned-slate telling and the two shut-room tellings on separate places, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes plus the loft scrape, the pale line and the held inch on six places. Six answers across six scenes, the **sixty-first** down at once, the **sixty-second** held for the small intake and down before the letting-go, the **sixty-third** kept to the river's own low interval with no count in the house to borrow, the **sixty-fourth** one borrowed rhythm crossed with one kept, the **sixty-fifth** one rhythm held through the little silence after Sella's count, and the **sixty-sixth** up an inch through the finger's-width door and carried through the small intake, the hold, the letting-go and the little silence of one of Sella's breaths in the room that does not touch, brought down on the river's next low note — two rhythms crossing in the inch, remembered, not a longer reach, miss entered, hearing, seventh, tenth stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule. Sixty-six answers stand on the sixth place, exact, complete and worth nothing. |

The hand went to the record before the person and to the pot not at all.
